### [1] [A Primal Perspective on Distributionally Robust Optimization: An Investigation on Modeling and Solution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04123)
*Yiqi Tian,Bo Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从原始视角研究分布鲁棒优化（DRO），提出了一种名为BiCS的算法框架，通过构造和利用"分布切割"在闭合（可能无界）样本空间上直接处理模糊集中的分布。该框架可应用于标准DRO、几乎必然DRO、带各类机会约束的DRO以及用局部信息增强的DRO，在矩和Wasserstein模糊集上表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单阶段DRO依赖对偶重构，但当模型或模糊集结构复杂时，对偶重构会变得困难甚至不可行。作者希望从原始视角出发，直接在模糊集中操作分布，从而规避对偶转换的复杂性。

Method: 提出BiCS算法框架，核心思想是在原始空间中通过迭代构造"分布切割"来逼近最优分布。该框架适用于多种DRO变体，包括标准DRO、几乎必然DRO、带机会约束的DRO，以及用局部信息强化模糊集的DRO，并能处理闭合但可能无界的样本空间。

Result: 在矩模糊集和Wasserstein模糊集上的数值实验表明，BiCS框架性能优越，能解决现有紧致重构方法不可用或计算困难的案例。在局部信息研究中，该方法还能直接呈现最坏情况分布的变化。

Conclusion: BiCS提供了一种从原始视角解决DRO的通用且高效的算法框架，拓宽了DRO的适用范围，尤其适用于对偶方法难以处理的结构复杂场景，并为模糊集的设计与分析提供了新的可视化解读视角。

Abstract: As a popular optimization scheme, distributionally robust optimization (DRO) protects decisions against ambiguity in probability distributions. For (single-stage) DRO, prevailing dual reformulations can become difficult when model or ambiguity-set structures are complex. We study DRO from a primal perspective, working directly with distributions in ambiguity sets on closed, potentially unbounded sample spaces. This perspective leads to an algorithmic framework, referred to as BiCS, that constructs and leverages distribution cuts to achieve strong performance. We show that BiCS is applicable to standard DRO, almost-sure DRO, DRO with various chance constraints, and DRO with ambiguity sets strengthened by local information. Numerical experiments with moment and Wasserstein ambiguity sets show that this framework demonstrates superior performance, including solving cases where the examined compact reformulations are unavailable or computationally difficult. The local-information study also makes changes in worst-case distributions directly visible.

</details>


### [2] [Evacuation Planning for Disaster Preparedness: An Adaptive Robust Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04225)
*Jaehyuk Kim,Merve Bodur,Maria E. Mayorga,Osman Y. Ozaltin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个自适应鲁棒疏散规划模型，联合优化避难所选址、疏散路线分配、救援物资预置和灾后救援物资分配，使用分区-定界算法在不确定性下求解，并验证了集中式路线规划相比分散式用户选择可将最坏情况下未满足需求和疏散时间分别降低最高90.6%和79.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害疏散规划需要在疏散人数及其空间分布未知的情况下做出关键决策（避难所选址、路线分配、物资预置），这些决策高度相互依赖，且需在最大化救援需求覆盖与最小化疏散时间之间取得平衡，现有工作缺乏统一的自适应鲁棒优化框架。

Method: 构建自适应鲁棒疏散规划模型，目标为最小化最坏情况下避难所物资未满足需求的加权和与拥堵依赖的疏散时间之和；该问题含混合整数补偿，作者刻画了其理论复杂度驱动因素，并提出分区-定界（partition-and-bound）算法，通过对不确定性集中最关键的子分区进行选择性划分来保持可操作性，同时生成强上下界；此外还构建了用户路线选择模型作为对比基准。

Result: 计算实验表明，集中式路线规划相比分散式用户选择可将最坏情况下的未满足需求降低最高90.6%、疏散时间降低最高79.3%；灾后自适应物资再分配进一步改善了救援需求覆盖；协调疏散路线与救援物资分配在不确定性下创造了可观的运营价值。

Conclusion: 集中式路线规划主要通过协调各避难所的疏散人流来缓解拥堵，而自适应再分配主要在救援物资稀缺或预置不灵活时提升需求覆盖；疏散路线与物资分配的协同在不确定性下具有显著运营价值。

Abstract: Evacuation planning for disaster preparedness requires making critical decisions under uncertainty before the number and spatial distribution of evacuees are known, including shelter location, evacuation route assignment, and relief supply prepositioning. Because these decisions are highly interdependent, planners must balance the competing objectives of maximizing relief demand coverage and minimizing evacuation time. We propose, to our knowledge, the first adaptive robust evacuation planning model to jointly optimize shelter locations, evacuation route assignments, relief supply prepositioning, and post-disaster relief item distribution. The model minimizes the worst-case weighted sum of unmet demand for relief items across shelters and the congestion-dependent evacuation time. We characterize theoretical complexity drivers of the resulting problem with mixed-integer recourse and develop a partition-and-bound algorithm that maintains tractability by selectively partitioning only the most critical subpartition of the uncertainty set while producing strong upper and lower bounds. To quantify the value of centralized route planning, we also formulate a user route choice alternative in which evacuees choose among acceptable routes. Computational experiments quantify the value of centralized route planning, which reduces worst-case unmet demand and evacuation time by up to 90.6\% and 79.3\%, respectively, relative to decentralized user route choice. Adaptive post-disaster supply redistribution further improves relief demand coverage. Coordination between evacuation routing and relief distribution creates substantial operational value under uncertainty. Centralized route planning primarily mitigates congestion by coordinating evacuee flows across shelters, whereas adaptive redistribution primarily improves relief demand coverage when relief supplies are scarce or inflexibly prepositioned.

</details>


### [3] [Strong error analysis for the stochastic momentum optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04245)
*Davide Gallon,Arnulf Jentzen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对动量随机梯度下降（momentum SGD）优化器进行了严格的误差分析，建立了关于学习率、小批量大小和单点凸性常数的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD及其变体（如Adam、AdamW、MUON等）是训练深度神经网络的核心优化方法，几乎所有这些流行的加速/自适应优化器都依赖于动量SGD。然而，动量SGD优化的严格误差分析仍然缺失，这限制了对其理论基础和收敛行为深入理解。

Method: 作者对动量SGD优化器建立了严格的收敛率分析，明确将收敛速度表征为学习率（步长）大小、小批量大小以及单点凸性常数三个关键参数的函数，提供了量化的误差界。

Result: 推导出了动量SGD优化器在单点凸性条件下的收敛率，揭示了收敛速度与学习率、小批量大小和凸性常数之间的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为动量SGD提供了严格的数学保证，加深了对其收敛特性的理解，为大规模AI系统的优化器选择与调参提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) optimization schemes are the methods of choice for the optimization of deep neural networks (DNNs) in artificial intelligence (AI) systems. Often not the standard SGD method is used but instead suitable accelerated, adaptive, and/or normalized variants of standard SGD such as Adam, AdamW, and MUON are employed to train large scale AI systems in practically relevant settings. The acceleration (higher order convergence speed) in all these popular optimizers relies on the momentum SGD optimizer. In this work we provide a rigorous error analysis for the momentum SGD optimizer. In particular, we establish convergence rates for the momentum optimizer in terms of the size of the learning rate (step size), the size of the mini-batch, and the size of the one-point convexity constant.

</details>


### [4] [Dynamic Dispatching for Time-Sensitive Blood Sample Collection and Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04275)
*Arash Dehghan,Aliaa Alnaggar,Mucahit Cevik,Merve Bodur*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究医院和诊断实验室中快递车队的实时调度问题，将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，并提出一种基于神经近似动态规划的框架。该方法通过双重价值函数分解分别表示车辆状态和采集中心状态，结合匹配优化选择调度动作。在大多伦多地区的仿真实验中，相比短视基线方法，该方法将样品按时送达比例提高1至9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院和诊断实验室依赖快递车队从分散的采集中心收集血液样本并在其短暂的有效期内送达分析。样本迟到会导致昂贵的重新采集并延误诊断。传统的静态规划模型在需求未知前固定路线，反应式启发式仅响应当前积压，都无法有效权衡即时采集与合并运输。因此需要一种能够预见未来到达需求的前瞻性实时调度方法。

Method: 将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，开发神经近似动态规划框架用于集中式调度。核心创新是双重价值函数分解：分别通过神经网络训练表示车辆状态和采集中心状态的估价函数，训练基于决策后状态。这些学习到的估计通过匹配优化整合，在选择调度动作时平衡供应可用性、需求紧迫性和下游机会成本。

Result: 在真实的多数伦多地区网络上的计算实验表明，与短视基线方法和消融变体相比，所提出的双重价值函数策略使样本按时交付比例提高1至9个百分点。在车队规模小、容量紧、截止期短和路由约束严格的条件下收益最大。按时交付的提升进而减少了对昂贵外部快递的依赖。

Conclusion: 基于神经近似动态规划的双重价值函数分解方法能够有效解决医疗样本快递车队的前瞻性实时调度问题，在严格约束条件下相比短视方法具有显著优势，并带来降低外部快递依赖的间接经济效益。

Abstract: Hospitals and diagnostic laboratories rely on couriers to collect blood samples from geographically dispersed collection centres and deliver them for analysis before their short viability windows expire; late deliveries force costly re-collection and can delay diagnosis. We study the real-time dispatching of such a courier fleet, in which sample requests arrive stochastically at the centres throughout the day and a central dispatcher must repeatedly decide which vehicles to send, which centres each should visit, and whether to collect urgent samples immediately or consolidate them into later trips, subject to hard delivery deadlines and vehicle capacity limits. Unlike static planning models, which fix routes before demand is known, and reactive heuristics, which respond only to the current backlog, our approach anticipates future arrivals when weighing immediate collection against consolidation. We formulate the problem as a Markov decision process and develop a neural approximate dynamic programming framework for centralized dispatch. The method introduces a dual value function decomposition that separately represents vehicle states and collection-centre states through neural networks trained on post-decision states. These learned estimates are integrated through a matching formulation that selects dispatch actions while balancing supply availability, demand urgency, and downstream opportunity cost. Computational experiments on a realistic Greater Toronto Area network compare the proposed policy with myopic baselines and ablation variants. Results show that the proposed dual value function policy raises the share of sample volume delivered on time by 1 to 9 percentage points over myopic baselines, with the largest gains under tight fleet, capacity, deadline, and routing constraints; these on-time gains, in turn, reduce reliance on costly external couriers.

</details>


### [5] [Achieving First-Order Statistical Improvements in Data-Driven Optimization: From No-Free-Lunch to Amplified Decision Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04312)
*Henry Lam,Tianyu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统性地剖析了"方向扰动的经验优化"（EO+）这一统摄性框架，该框架涵盖正则化、分布鲁棒优化、迁移学习及情境优化等众多数据驱动优化方法。作者证明：在没有正确设定的额外补充信息时，任何EO+方法最多只能带来二阶改进（"没有免费午餐"）；而当利用几何上有效的补充信息时，通过选择比文献中典型建议显著更大的超参数，可以实现一阶改进。文章还提出基于超额风险估计（利用系统知识或自助重采样）的原则性方法来最大化一阶增益，并揭示了该增益与蒙特卡洛模拟中的控制变量原理之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的优化方法近年来大量涌现，但许多方法仅凭鲁棒性或正则化的直觉动机而提出，其统计收益往往不清晰，且即使有结果也通常是逐案建立的。本文旨在提供一个统一的系统性框架来理解这些方法，并回答一个根本性问题：这些方法的统计改进能力究竟上限在哪里，以及在什么条件下才能实现实质性的改进。

Method: 作者提出了"方向扰动经验优化"（EO+）作为统摄性框架，将多种数据驱动优化方法统一视角。通过理论分析，他们论证了：(1) 在没有正确指定额外信息时，EO+最多只能提供二阶改进；(2) 当利用几何上有效的补充信息时可以达成一阶改进，但需要选择远大于文献建议的超参数。此外，基于超额风险估计（通过系统知识或自助重采样）构建了原理性方法以最大化一阶增益，并将其与控制变量原理（蒙特卡洛方差缩减技术）建立联系。

Result: 本文的主要结果包括：(1) 建立了"无免费午餐"定理——没有正确指定的补充信息时，EO+方法最多只能实现二阶改进；(2) 证明了利用几何上有效的补充信息时可以实现一阶改进，且需要显著更大的超参数；(3) 提出了基于超额风险估计的原则性方法（系统知识或自助重采样）来最大化一阶增益；(4) 揭示了该方法与蒙特卡洛控制变量原理之间的深层联系，从而解释了为何几何上有效的补充信息是必要条件。

Conclusion: 本文通过EO+统一框架对数据驱动优化方法进行了系统性剖析，给出了统计改进能力的清晰上界。核心结论是：没有正确补充信息则无免费午餐（最多二阶改进），而正确的几何有效补充信息配合大幅调优超参数才能实现一阶改进。此外，超额风险估计方法论与控制变量原理的联系为实际应用提供了明确指导。

Abstract: Recent proliferation of data-optimization integration has led to a range of methods that aim to improve the statistical performance of data-driven optimization decisions. However, while many of these methods are motivated intuitively from a robustness or regularization perspective, their resulting statistical benefits are often unclear and, even if available, are established on a case-by-case basis. We provide a systematic dissection of data-driven optimization formulations using the view of "directionally perturbed" empirical optimization (EO). Specifically, this umbrella of formulations, which we call "EO+", covers many existing data-driven optimization methods, including regularization, distributionally robust optimization, transfer learning, and analogous methods for contextual optimization. On the one hand, we argue that without additional, correctly specified, side information, any EO+ method can result in at most second-order improvements. This provides a negative conclusion, namely ``no free lunch is possible", on the statistical power of EO+. On the other hand, we show that when leveraging side information that is geometrically effective, achieving first-order improvements is possible by choosing hyperparameters that are significantly larger than what is typically suggested in the literature. Moreover, we construct a principled methodology based on excess risk estimation, via either system knowledge or bootstrap resampling, to maximize the first-order gain. We demonstrate how this gain connects to the control-variate principle, a variance reduction technique in the Monte Carlo simulation literature, which helps explain why geometrically effective side information is necessary.

</details>


### [6] [A Linearly Convergent Projection-Free Algorithm for Smooth Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04321)
*Elad Hazan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑强凸函数在有界凸集上的最小化问题。当可行集是光滑的时，投影梯度下降中的投影运算可以被一次梯度计算和一次支撑切平面（supporting-tangent）计算所替代，同时保持线性收敛速度。此外，所需的切平面可以通过约Õ(d)次隶属度查询（membership oracle queries）近似到足够精度，其中d为环境维度。此前，无投影线性收敛仅对多面体集或同时光滑且强凸的集合成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影梯度下降虽然在线性收敛速度上表现良好，但每次迭代都需要对可行集进行投影，而投影运算在大规模或复杂可行集上计算代价往往非常高昂。因此，研究者希望设计无投影（projection-free）的替代方案，在保持线性收敛率的同时降低每次迭代的计算成本。

Method: 作者证明了当可行集为光滑集时，可以将投影替换为一次梯度计算和一次支撑切平面计算。每个迭代中所需的支撑切平面可以通过约Õ(d)次隶属度查询来近似到足够精度，从而在不进行精确投影的情况下保持线性收敛性。

Result: 对于光滑可行集上的光滑强凸函数最小化问题，提出的无投影方法保持了线性收敛速率，且每次迭代仅需一次梯度计算和一次支撑切平面计算。切平面可通过约Õ(d)次隶属度查询近似获得。

Conclusion: 该结果将无投影线性收敛的适用范围从多面体集或光滑且强凸的集合，推广到更一般的光滑凸集，显著拓宽了无投影优化方法的应用场景。

Abstract: We consider minimizing a smooth, strongly convex function over a convex set. Projected gradient descent is known to converge linearly in this setting, but each iteration requires a projection onto the feasible set, which may be computationally expensive.
  We show that when the feasible set is smooth, projection can be replaced by one gradient computation and a single supporting-tangent computation per iteration, while preserving linear convergence. Moreover, the required tangent can be approximated to sufficient accuracy using $\widetilde O(d)$ membership-oracle queries, where $d$ is the ambient dimension. Previously, projection-free linear convergence was known only for polyhedral sets or for sets that are both smooth and strongly convex.

</details>


### [7] [An $α$-Potential Game Approach to $N$-Player Stochastic Linear-Quadratic Differential Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04386)
*Chenhui Hao,Jingtao Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从α-势博弈的角度研究N人随机线性二次（LQ）微分博弈。首先推导了带乘性噪声的闭环LQ博弈中代价函数一阶和二阶线性导数的概率表示与PDE表示并证明其等价性，然后构建开环随机LQ α-势博弈框架，通过线性导数构造α势函数并给出逼近参数α的显式上界，最后通过扩增变分过程将α势函数最小化转化为有限维随机控制问题，从而得到开环α-Nash均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有N人随机LQ微分博弈的直接求解通常计算复杂或难以处理，势博弈理论提供了一种通过极小化单一势函数来求解均衡的有效途径。然而，经典的势博弈框架针对一般随机LQ博弈并不直接适用，需要引入α-势博弈的近似概念并为其建立系统化的构造方法。

Method: （1）对带乘性噪声的闭环LQ博弈，分别从概率方法和PDE方法推导代价函数的一阶与二阶线性导数表示，并证明二者等价。（2）在此基础上构建开环随机LQ α-势博弈框架，利用线性导数构造α势函数，推导α的显式上界（依赖模型系数与容许控制半径）。（3）通过将变分过程增广到状态中，把α势函数的最小化转化为有限维随机LQ控制问题，从而求得开环α-Nash均衡。（4）应用于网络LQ博弈，证明所得反馈表示与条件McKean-Vlasov方法一致。

Result: 成功建立了闭环LQ博弈中线性导数的概率表示与PDE表示的等价性；构建了开环随机LQ α-势博弈框架并给出α的显式上界；通过有限维随机控制问题的转化获得了开环α-Nash均衡；在应用部分验证了方法得到的反馈表示与现有条件McKean-Vlasov方法的反馈一致，且推导过程更加直接。

Conclusion: 本文为N人随机LQ微分博弈提供了一种系统的α-势博弈求解框架，通过线性导数构造势函数并将问题简化为有限维LQ控制，既保证了均衡的逼近质量（α有显式界），又在应用上与已有方法相互印证且推导更简洁。

Abstract: This paper studies $N$-player stochastic linear-quadratic (LQ) differential games from the perspective of $α$-potential games. We first consider a closed-loop LQ game with multiplicative noise, where both the drift and the diffusion coefficients depend linearly on the state and the full control vector. For this model, we derive probabilistic and partial differential equation (PDE) representations for the first- and second-order linear derivatives of the players' cost function and prove the equivalence between them. Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) optimization methods are the standard instruments for the training of deep neural networks (DNNs). In many relevant artificial intelligence (AI) systems - such as popular large language models (LLMs)-not the standard SGD scheme is used as the optimization method but instead suitable accelerated variants of SGD are employed. One of the most popular methods of such accelerated SGD variants is the momentum orthogonalized by Newton-Schulz (MUON) optimizer proposed by Jordan et al. in 2024. The MUON optimizer exploits the special matrix structure of the weight parameters in the training of the DNNs and, in its original form, employs five Newton-Schultz (NS) matrix steps in each MUON iteration.

The considered optimizer covers MUON with the original NS polynomial as well as MUON combined with the recently proposed Polar Express method as special cases. For a simple class of stochastic optimization problems (SOPs) we show for almost every mini-batch size that MUON fails to converge to the solution of the SOP as the number of gradient steps converges to infinity. We also establish an error analysis for MUON with the generalized NS steps that provides convergence rates in terms of the number of gradient steps and in terms of the size of the mini-batch. We illustrate our general error analysis for MUON in the case of several concrete examples including quadratic stochastic optimization problems (SOPs) as well as $\ell_2$ regularized logistic regression for binary classification.

TL;DR: 本文针对Shannon熵核下的Bregman投影梯度法（BPGM）迭代收敛这一长期未解难题，提出了全新的收敛分析框架。核心创新在于引入了“缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质”这一概念，证明了该性质能确保BPGM迭代收敛，且对所有连续次解析函数成立；进一步证明了当SKŁ指数为1/2时，BPGM具有线性收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出“缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质”这一新概念，用于刻画函数在Bregman几何下的局部增长行为。基于此性质建立BPGM的迭代收敛分析框架，并证明SKŁ性质对连续次解析函数的普适性。同时通过严格互补条件和目标梯度局部Lipschitz连续性，证明KŁ指数1/2可蕴含SKŁ指数1/2，从而给出线性收敛实例。

Result: 证明了SKŁ性质能确保BPGM迭代收敛，且对所有连续次解析函数成立；当问题具有SKŁ指数1/2时，BPGM序列展现线性收敛；给出了满足SKŁ指数1/2的函数实例。

Abstract: The iterate convergence of \textit{Bregman projected gradient method} (BPGM) has remained a long-standing open problem, especially for the widely adopted Shannon entropy kernel. Existing convergence results are often limited, relying on Lipschitz continuity of the kernel's gradient or restrictive conditions on the objective function. In this paper, we develop a novel convergence analysis framework for the BPGM with the Shannon entropy kernel, yielding strong convergence results for a broad class of objective functions under linear constraints. The cornerstone of our framework is a new concept called \textit{scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz} (SKŁ) property, which captures the local growth behavior of a function under the Bregman geometry. We show that the SKŁ property ensures the iterate convergence of BPGM and holds for all continuous subanalytical functions. Furthermore, we prove that the BPGM sequence exhibits linear convergence if the problem possesses an SKŁ exponent of $1/2$. We then furnish the examples of functions with the SKŁ exponent $1/2$ by proving that the SKŁ exponent $1/2$ is implied by the KŁ exponent $1/2$ under strict complementarity and local Lipschitz continuity of the objective's gradient. Building on these novel results, our work takes a first step towards resolving the open problem of BPGM iterate convergence.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 从统计学习理论出发，搭建一个"目的-手段"（means-ends）论证：如果要获得理论可靠性和"所见即所得"（what-you-see-is-what-you-get）的保证，就必须在拟合与简单性之间实施特定的权衡（即正则化）。文中区分了三种可能的证明路径：纯粹实用主义的（为简单而简单）、本体论的（真即是简单的），以及本文所主张的方法论的（为获得可靠性保证而必须偏好简单性）。

Result: 得出一个真正的方法论层面的合理性证明：正则化（即用简单性换取拟合度的权衡）是获得统计学习理论所承诺的可靠性保证的必要条件。该论证既没有退化为"为简单性而简单性"的纯粹实用主义原则，也没有依赖"真理是简单的"这一本体论假设。

Conclusion: 奥卡姆剃刀原则可以通过统计学习理论获得一个坚实的方法论基础：对简单性的偏好不是出于审美或形而上学的理由，而是获取理论可靠性保证的逻辑必经之路。

Abstract: The principle of Occam's razor, which instructs us to prefer simplicity in inductive inference, has attracted much scrutiny both in the philosophy of science and in machine learning. In either field, however, a justification for the principle has been elusive. In this paper, building on an earlier "core argument," I spell out a justification from statistical learning theory for the procedure of regularization: for trading off fit for simplicity. The means-ends argument is that in order to profit from theoretical reliability and "what-you-see-is-what-you-get" guarantees, one must implement a certain preference for simplicity over fit. This is a genuine methodological justification, which neither collapses to a purely pragmatic principle that we prefer simplicity for its own sake, nor to an ontological assumption that the truth is simple.

</details>

*Teruyuki Katsuoka,Tomohiro Shiraishi,Shuichi Nishino,Ichiro Takeuchi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AutoSI是一个自动化的选择性推断（Selective Inference）框架，能够自动从算法操作中构建选择事件，无需专家手工推导，并将可处理的选择事件范围从线性/二次不等式扩展到任意有理函数表达的数据算法，同时保证有限样本下p值的精确有效性。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: AutoSI框架通过两个关键手段解决上述问题：(1) 从算法的逐操作（individual operations）自动构建选择事件，用户只需以NumPy-like代码编写算法即可；(2) 将SI可处理的选择事件从线性/二次不等式扩展到任意可由数据有理函数（多项式之比）表达的算法。论文证明了AutoSI计算的p值在有限样本下精确有效。

Result: AutoSI在三个特征选择方法上得到验证，每个方法仅需几十行代码。其中一个方法——通过交叉验证R²选择调参参数的lasso——在现有精确SI框架中无法处理，而AutoSI使其成为可能。合成数据和真实数据实验表明，生成的p值在标称水平下控制了I类错误率（假阳性率），同时保持了较高功效。

Conclusion: AutoSI大幅降低了选择性推断的使用门槛，实现了对更广泛算法类别的精确SI，使得原本需要领域专家手工推导的复杂过程变得自动化，拓展了精确SI的实际应用范围。

Abstract: Selective inference (SI) provides statistically valid $p$-values for hypotheses selected by applying an algorithm to the data, correcting for the bias that arises when the same data are used both to select and to test a hypothesis. Developing an SI procedure for a new algorithm, however, has required an expert to derive, and then implement, the selection event, i.e., the conditions under which the hypothesis is selected. Repeating this specialized effort for every new algorithm is why exact SI has so far been available for only a narrow class. We propose AutoSI, a framework that removes this barrier in two ways. First, AutoSI constructs the selection event automatically from the algorithm's individual operations, so the user only writes the algorithm as ordinary NumPy-like code and derives nothing by hand. Second, AutoSI broadens the class of selection events SI can handle: existing exact methods are limited to selection events characterized by linear or quadratic inequalities in the data, whereas AutoSI covers any algorithm expressible through rational functions of the data (ratios of polynomials). We prove that the $p$-values computed by AutoSI are exactly valid in finite samples. We demonstrate AutoSI on three feature-selection methods, each written in a few dozen lines of code. One of these methods, the lasso with its tuning parameter selected by cross-validated $R^2$, cannot be handled within existing exact SI frameworks and is made possible by AutoSI. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that the resulting $p$-values control the type I error rate (i.e., the false positive rate) at the nominal level while retaining high power.

</details>

*Yizhu Wang,Mu Niu,Xiaochen Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出内在混合潜扩散模型（ILDM），通过将潜空间解释为未知黎曼流形上的坐标图，并用概率解码器量化几何与不确定性，在混合扩散过程中根据局部不确定性在黎曼与欧几里得动力学之间切换，从而在数据稀疏场景下提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型在高维数据合成中虽取得最优结果，但依赖大规模训练数据且忽略内在几何结构；潜扩散模型虽降低维度，却强加欧几里得结构，无法捕捉底层流形的几何特性，尤其在高维数据稀疏场景下问题突出。作者旨在同时解决高维性与几何建模两大痛点。

Method: ILDM将潜空间视为未知黎曼流形的坐标图，通过概率解码器量化几何与不确定性。前向过程为混合扩散，根据局部不确定性在黎曼与欧几里得动力学之间切换，其中黎曼分量由解码器导出的概率度量张量控制。为学习生成动力学，提出适配混合扩散设置的近似去噪分数匹配方法，使反向过程由混合朗之万动力学定义。

Abstract: We introduce the Intrinsic Hybrid Latent Diffusion Model (ILDM), a generative framework that integrates probabilistic dimensionality reduction with geometry-aware diffusion on unknown manifolds. While diffusion models (DMs) have achieved state-of-the-art results in high-dimensional data synthesis, they rely on large training datasets and ignore intrinsic geometric structure. Latent diffusion models (LDMs) address the high dimensionality by learning a latent space, but they typically impose a Euclidean structure, failing to capture the underlying manifold geometry, especially problematic in data-sparse regimes. ILDM addresses these limitations by interpreting the latent space as a chart of an unknown Riemannian manifold, with geometry and uncertainty quantified through a probabilistic decoder. The forward process is a hybrid diffusion that switches between Riemannian and Euclidean dynamics based on local uncertainty, where the Riemannian component is governed by a probabilistic metric tensor derived from the decoder. To learn the generative dynamics, we introduce an approximate denoising score matching method tailored to the hybrid diffusion setting, enabling a backward process defined by hybrid Langevin dynamics. Experiments on COIL-100, MNIST, and cardiac MRI datasets demonstrate that ILDM significantly improves generation quality, achieving lower FID and LPIPS scores compared to standard diffusion and latent diffusion models.

</details>

Conclusion: 该研究在理论上纠偏了SCMS算法的收敛目标，提出了"稳定脊"这一全新概念以替代静态脊，并给出了更高效、统计一致的广义SCMS框架，为密度脊提取领域提供了坚实的理论基矗。

Abstract: The Subspace Constrained Mean Shift (SCMS) algorithm is a popular nonparametric method for extracting density ridges, which serve as a low-dimensional representation of high-dimensional data. It is a widely held belief in the literature that SCMS trajectories converge to the classical density ridge, which we call the "static ridge", defined via the density gradient and the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the density's Hessian. In this paper, we demonstrate that this assumption does not hold in general, as the static definition fails to account for the rotation of the trailing eigenspace along the continuous flow of the algorithm's underlying vector field. To resolve this, we propose a paradigm shift by introducing the "stable ridge", a novel geometric structure defined through the lens of dynamical systems and the Jacobian of the projected density gradient. We prove that this stable ridge is the true theoretical target of the SCMS algorithm. Building upon this foundation, we develop a generalized SCMS framework utilizing a constant step size, establishing its uniform R-linear convergence and topological surjectivity onto the stable ridge. We further derive the rates of convergence for estimating the stable ridge in terms of the Hausdorff distance. Finally, we expose that the original SCMS algorithm suffers from polynomial-time computational complexity, which is caused by implicitly coupling the step size to the smoothing bandwidth via the Mean Shift operator, and demonstrate how our generalized framework provides a statistically consistent and more efficient solution.

</details>

*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在多个MERC基准数据集上的大量实验表明，C2MOE在各种缺失模态设置下均持续超越现有最先进方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [22] [Informational Frustration in Neural Manifolds: Shannon Bottlenecks and the Limits of Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30512)
*Srinivasa Rao P.,Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，将信息论、拓扑学和统计力学相结合，通过"熵可学习性地平线"(ELH)定律来映射深度学习的学习极限，并推导出Shannon-拓扑瓶颈定理，解释了grokking现象的本质，提出了熵梯度下降(EGD)算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论框架（如VC维度和Rademacher复杂度）无法解释过参数化深度网络出色的泛化能力，理论预测与实际表现之间存在巨大鸿沟，需要一个统一的理论框架来弥合这一差异。

Method: 构建了一个跨学科统一框架：(1) 提出熵可学习性地平线(ELH)定律，将数据流形的Shannon熵、决策边界的拓扑熵和权重空间的von Neumann熵联系起来；(2) 推导Shannon-拓扑瓶颈定理，刻画泛化失败的熵相变条件；(3) 将grokking重新解释为"熵释放"过程；(4) 提出熵梯度下降(EGD)优化算法，通过动态管理权重熵来维持学习能力。

Result: 通过该框架证明了当决策边界的几何复杂度超过信息地平线时，系统发生熵相变并陷入"信息挫折"（玻璃态刚性记忆）阶段，此时泛化在热力学意义上不可实现；grokking被解释为权重的突然重排以解锁瓶颈；EGD算法通过动态管理权重熵保持学习过程。

Conclusion: 该工作将熵重新定位为决定机器能否学习的根本物理量，不仅仅是追踪不确定性的工具，而是深度学习的核心物理机制。

Abstract: Why overparameterised deep networks generalise so remarkably well remains one of the most stubborn open questions in machine learning theory. Classical frameworks like VC dimension and Rademacher complexity predict catastrophic overfitting in modern models, leaving a massive theoretical gap between theory and reality. In this paper, we bridge this divide by introducing a unified framework that links information theory, topology, and statistical mechanics to map the hard limits of deep learning. Central to our approach is the Entropic Learnability Horizon (ELH): a fundamental law stating that a network can only truly learn a target function if the Shannon entropy of the data manifold outpaces the topological entropy of the function's decision boundary, balanced by the von Neumann entropy of the network's weight space. We establish the Shannon-Topological Bottleneck Theorem, proving that when a target boundary's geometric complexity exceeds this informational horizon, the system undergoes a sudden entropic phase transition. It falls into a state of Informational Frustration - a glassy, rigid memorization phase where generalization becomes thermodynamically impossible. Using this lens, we show that the enigmatic phenomenon of "grokking" is actually an Entropic Release, where weights abruptly reorganise to unlock the bottleneck. Finally, we translate this theory into practice with Entropic Gradient Descent (EGD), an optimization algorithm that dynamically manages weight entropy to keep learning on track. Ultimately, this work repositions entropy not just as a tool for tracking uncertainty but as the fundamental physical currency that dictates whether a machine can learn.

</details>


### [23] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了次优（minmax）超度量算子的ℓ₀型稳定性理论，分析稀疏扰动如何通过最小生成树（MST）传播，给出每个编辑的暴露割分数和树全局包络，并证明Hamming-Lipschitz界限及锐化结果。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的超度量稳定性理论通常基于ℓ∞或Gromov-Hausdorff度量，但这些界不适用于只改变少量成对距离的稀疏扰动场景。需要一种更适合稀疏编辑的稳定性理论来刻画影响范围。

Method: 通过ℓ₀型分析，证明稀疏编辑仅在最小生成树路径上传播：成对超度量值仅在树路径跨越被编辑的边或新暴露的割时改变。提出每编辑暴露割分数和树专属全局包络，证明Hamming-Lipschitz界限；并通过严格割分离和离树编辑的显式构造族证明锐化结果；同时证明多编辑条件下的条件近可加性原理。

Result: 在严格割分离下树边界精确达到；单个离树编辑可改变Θ(n²)个超度量条目；多编辑在认证的大变更区域和可忽略的重叠下满足近可加性。实验表明结构性分数对层次表示的脆弱性诊断有效。

Conclusion: 超度量对稀疏扰动的稳定性本质上由树几何决定，这种依赖不可避免。所提出的结构性分数可作为层次表示（如深度嵌入图）的脆弱性诊断工具。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计力学将权重分布约束视为对解空间的限制，从而降低模型容量。作者提出相反的视角：将规定的权重分布定义为学习在其上自然展开的内在几何，而非单纯的限制条件，从而在经典和量子学习系统中重新理解分布约束的作用。

Method: (1) 将深度神经网络公式化为各层经典Wasserstein空间的乘积流上的梯度流，将变分量子电路公式化为量子Wasserstein空间上的梯度流；(2) 把容量缩减重新解释为约束流形本身的度量结构；(3) 发展深度网络的分层平均场描述，并将框架扩展至使用1阶量子Wasserstein距离的量子设定；(4) 提出两种实用算法——Hierarchical DisCo-SGD和Quantum DisCo——沿乘积流形上的近似测地线进行优化。

Result: 在教师-学生问题、标准图像分类任务和小型变分量子分类器上的实验表明：遵从分布几何相比无约束和纯范数约束基线，改进了泛化性能、稳定了训练过程，并减轻了贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）的严重程度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将结构约束重新表述为几何先验，为将生物、谱或硬件衍生的分布信息纳入经典和量子学习系统提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: Normally the statistical mechanics of learning treats constraints on weight distributions as restrictions that shrink the space of possible solutions. Therefore, it reduces model capacity. In this paper we would like to take a contrary approach, which, however, is based on the earlier work on distribution-constrained perceptrons. Rather than treating a prescribed weight distribution as a mere restriction, we propose that it defines the intrinsic geometry upon which learning naturally unfolds. We formulate both deep neural networks and variational quantum circuits as gradient flows on a product of Wasserstein manifolds -- one classical Wasserstein space for each layer and one quantum Wasserstein space for the circuit parameters. Within this geometry, the capacity reduction, which was previously associated with distributional constraints, appears as the metric structure of the constraint manifold itself. We develop a hierarchical mean-field description for deep networks, extend the framework to the quantum setting using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order 1, and introduce two such practical algorithms, Hierarchical DisCo-SGD and Quantum DisCo, that follow approximate geodesics on the manifold of the product itself. Experiments on teacher-student problems, standard image classification tasks, and small variational quantum classifiers show that respecting these distributional geometries improves generalization, stabilizes training, and reduces the severity of barren plateaus compared with unconstrained and purely norm-based baselines. This approach firstly reframes structural constraints as geometric priors and suggests a route for incorporating biological, spectral, or hardware-derived distributional information into both learning systems, viz., classical and quantum learning.

</details>

*Oluwasegun A. Somefun*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Adam等自适应矩估计优化器为何有效是深度学习理论中的一个核心问题。作者希望建立一个统一的框架，能够从第一性原理推导出自适应优化中的多种关键机制（矩估计归一化、学习率调度、动量、谱归一化），从而为理解和设计更优的优化算法提供理论基础。

Method: 作者构建了一个信任域框架，其中每个权重更新步长被约束在一个由p阶矩（p∈[2,4]）约束所控制的信任域内。由此推导出一族学习率机制，基于二阶矩估计和归一化的p阶矩估计。当p=4时涉及峰度类估计。该通用机制被命名为Gmake，提供了一个统一视角来解读归一化、调度、动量和谱归一化。

Result: 在GPT2-124M（在FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories数据集上训练）上的实验表明：当信任域约束较弱时，四阶矩（p=4）实现收益最大；但随着信任域控制逐步加强，二阶矩（p=2）实现变得更具竞争力，通常能达到略低于四阶矩实现的验证损失。

Conclusion: 信任域框架为理解自适应矩估计优化器提供了统一的理论视角。四阶矩（峰度类）估计在弱约束下更有优势，而二阶矩估计在强约束下更为实用。这为在不同优化场景中选择合适的矩阶数提供了指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation. The general mechanism, referred to as \textsc{Gmake}, provides a unified interpretation of normalization by moment estimation, learning-rate scheduling, spectral lowpass filtering as momentum, and operator-level spectral normalization within a common trust-region framework. Experiments on GPT2-124M trained on FineWeb-Edu and TinyStories suggest that the fourth-moment realization provides its greatest benefit when trust-region constraints are weak. As progressively stronger trust-region controls are introduced, the second-moment realization becomes increasingly competitive, often achieving slightly lower validation loss than its corresponding fourth-moment realization.

</details>


### [26] [Sample Complexity of Multicalibration for Multilevel Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04288)

TL;DR: 本文研究了k个顺序依赖属性的多校准问题，在任意k≥2下给出了匹配的上下界样本复杂度（含对数因子），确立了Θ(ε^{-(k+2)})的样本复杂度，并应用于贝叶斯对、方差、偏度、条件风险价值等典型实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多校准要求预测器在多个群体上同时满足无偏性保证。许多预测任务需要预测同一条件结果分布的多个相关特征（如均值、方差、偏度、条件风险价值），其中每个属性在前序属性确定后才可识别。已有工作缺乏对这种顺序依赖属性序列的多校准样本复杂度分析。

Method: 论文建立了一个统一的框架，研究了k个属性的顺序多校准问题（每个属性一旦前序属性固定即可识别）。采用了随机化学习器算法，并推导了匹配的下界（通过构造仅有polylog多个二元群的困难实例）和上界（随机化学习器使用O(ε^{-(k+2)} + ε^{-2}log|G|)个样本）。

Result: 对于任意固定k≥2，在正则条件下建立了匹配的上下界样本复杂度（差对数因子）。即使只有polylog个二元群，达到多校准误差ε也需要Ω̃(ε^{-(k+2)})个样本；而对任意有限群族G，随机化学习器仅需O(ε^{-(k+2)} + ε^{-2}log|G|)个样本。因此对多项式大小群族，样本复杂度为Θ̃(ε^{-(k+2)})。论文在三个典型实例上验证了理论。

Conclusion: 顺序多校准的样本复杂度随属性数量k增加而急剧增长（ε指数为-(k+2)），反映了预测多个有序分布特征的内在困难。该框架推广了Bayes配对，但不要求属性源于单一损失函数，为多特征校准提供了统一的理论保证。

Abstract: Calibration requires a predictor to be unbiased after conditioning on its own predictions. Multicalibration asks for this guarantee simultaneously across a collection of groups. Many prediction tasks ask for several related features of the same conditional outcome distribution: variance is defined relative to the mean, skewness relative to both mean and variance, and conditional value at risk relative to a quantile. We study multicalibration for a sequence of $k$ properties in which each property is identifiable once the preceding properties are fixed. This framework includes Bayes pairs but does not require the properties to arise from a single loss.
  For every fixed $k\ge2$, we establish matching upper and lower sample-complexity bounds up to logarithmic factors under regularity conditions. Even with only polylogarithmically many binary groups, achieving multicalibration error $\varepsilon$ requires $\widetildeΩ(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)})$ samples. Conversely, for any finite group family $\mathcal G$, we give a randomized learner using $O(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)}+\varepsilon^{-2}\log|\mathcal G|)$ samples. Thus the sample complexity is $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-(k+2)})$ for polynomial-size group families. We instantiate the theory for three canonical examples.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用全卷积神经网络（FCN）对透射谱中每个数据点进行分类，划分为共振区域或非共振区域。模型在七条透射谱上训练/测试，包括两条评估谱和五条实验谱，并基于此前PHYSOR 2026的分析扩展了训练数据。

Result: 模型在已见数据上达到约93%的分类准确率，但该指标不能真实反映其泛化能力。即使增加了额外训练数据，模型仍无法可靠地推广到此前未见过的同位素。

Conclusion: 当前基于FCN的中子共振自动检测方法尚不足以替代人工依赖R-Matrix分析。未来工作应探索更大、更多样化的训练数据集，并融入中子共振的已知物理特性（如能级间距、宽度分布等）以提升模型的可推广性和可靠性。

Abstract: This work investigates the feasibility of augmenting traditional R-Matrix codes with a robust machine learning framework for automatically detecting neutron resonances in transmission spectra. Neutron transmission data are often complex and noisy, making them difficult to analyze using traditional peak-identification methods. The state-of-the-art R-Matrix codes currently used by physicists to fit these data often depend on prior evaluations and require substantial manual effort. This preliminary study demonstrates a method for accelerating the post-experimental processing of neutron transmission data and reducing bias associated with dependence on prior evaluations. We employ a fully convolutional neural network to classify individual points as belonging to resonance or non-resonance regions in seven transmission spectra---two evaluated and five experimental. Although the model achieves classification accuracies in the range of 93\%, further analysis shows that this metric overstates its ability to generalize. Building on our prior analysis in PHYSOR 2026, we find that, despite the inclusion of additional training data, the method does not generalize reliably to previously unseen isotopes. To address these limitations, future work should evaluate whether a larger and more diverse training dataset can produce a generalizable model and should incorporate known physical characteristics of neutron resonances to improve model performance.

</details>


### [28] [ArborEnum: Decision Tree Rashomon Sets over Continuous Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04310)
*Zakk Heile,Hayden McTavish,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了第一个能够精确枚举连续特征决策树Rashomon集的算法，利用连续特征的有序结构而非依赖数据二值化，并开发了近似枚举与任意时间算法，在保持高召回率的同时实现了数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rashomon效应描述了多个模型在相同任务上表现近乎相同性能的现象，这对鲁棒性、特征重要性和可定制性有重要影响。计算决策树的Rashomon集（所有正则化损失接近最优的模型集合）具有重要应用价值，但现有方法都依赖于数据的二值化处理，要么限制树可用的分割点，要么显著增加本已困难的组合问题的复杂度。因此需要一种能直接利用连续特征有序结构的精确枚举算法。

Method: 提出首个直接利用连续特征有序结构进行精确枚举决策树Rashomon集的算法。具体包括：(1) 精确枚举算法，利用连续特征的有序特性避免二值化；(2) 近似枚举的松弛方法；(3) 任意时间算法，逐步精炼候选阈值集合，生成越来越详细的近似结果，最终收敛到连续特征的Rashomon集。

Result: 实验表明，粗糙的二值化会遗漏大量决策树、重要特征以及预测多样性。所提出的算法相较于现有枚举方法实现了数量级的加速，近似方法在保持近完美召回率的同时进一步提升了速度。

Conclusion: 这项工作首次解决了连续特征下决策树Rashomon集精确枚举的难题，消除了对数据预二值化的依赖，并通过近似与任意时间框架在效率与完整性之间取得了良好平衡，为Rashomon集的实用计算提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: The Rashomon effect describes the phenomenon that many models can achieve nearly equivalent performance on the same learning task, with significant ramifications for robustness, feature importance, and customizability. These use cases motivate the computation of Rashomon sets: the set of all models whose regularized loss is near-optimal. Decision trees are one of the few model classes for which Rashomon sets can be fully enumerated, but this computation has always been conditional on a binarization of the original data, either restricting which splits each tree is allowed to make or substantially increasing the complexity of an already difficult combinatorial problem. We introduce the first algorithm that exactly enumerates decision-tree Rashomon sets while exploiting the ordered structure of continuous features. We further develop a relaxation for approximate enumeration and an anytime algorithm that progressively refines the set of candidate thresholds, producing increasingly detailed approximations that converge to the continuous-feature Rashomon set. Experiments show that coarse binarization can miss many trees, important features, and predictive multiplicity; our algorithms achieve orders-of-magnitude speedups over existing enumeration methods, with approximations providing further speedups while maintaining near-perfect recall.

</details>


### [29] [Lindblad-Inspired Multi-Timescale Reservoir Computing with Separable Rotation and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04028)
*Jyotiranjan Beuria,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于经典Lindblad启发式多时间尺度储层计算框架，通过精确离散化的阻尼旋转模式构造循环算子，使旋转（相位混合）和衰减（记忆遗忘）成为独立可控的设计变量，并直接提供全局回波状态稳定性保证，在多项基准上达到最佳或竞争力水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回波状态网络（ESN）将所有信号混合、记忆保留和稳定性问题挤在一个随机循环矩阵中，现有结构化设计虽然改善了拓扑、范数保持、泄漏或深度，但无法对可逆混合和不可逆遗忘进行独立的模态控制，也无法直接给出全局稳定性保证。

Method: 借鉴开放量子系统的Lindblad主方程思想，构建经典多时间尺度储层。循环算子由精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态组装而成，旋转与衰减成为独立设计变量。正交模态混合保持矩阵正规性（normality），衰减谱直接决定回波状态稳定性裕度，无需事后谱半径缩放。

Result: 在10个对齐随机种子上，与标准、泄漏、深度、正交、循环和下一代储层及紧凑训练GRU对比，涵盖线性记忆、非线性递归、混沌预测、延迟逻辑和真实传感器校准。所提储层在受限NARMA-20上取得最佳固定储层性能，在Lorenz-63上取得最低平均误差，在最强线性记忆基准上持平最佳结果，整体保持广泛竞争力。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明旋转增加状态多样性而耗散提供受控遗忘并改善预测条件数。该框架提供可解释的循环架构，混合、记忆和稳定性为显式且可独立调整的设计变量。

Abstract: Echo-state networks enable efficient temporal learning by fixing the recurrent dynamics and training only a linear readout. However, conventional reservoirs typically accommodate signal mixing, memory retention, and stability within a single random recurrent matrix. Existing structured designs improve topology, norm preservation, leakage, or depth, but generally do not provide separate modal control of reversible mixing and irreversible forgetting together with a direct global stability guarantee. We introduce a classical Lindblad-inspired multi-timescale reservoir that bridges open-system dynamical principles with structured state-space modeling. The recurrent operator is assembled from exactly discretized damped rotational modes, so rotation and decay become independent design variables governing phase mixing and memory loss. Orthogonal mode mixing preserves normality, while the decay spectrum directly determines the echo-state stability margin without post-hoc spectral-radius rescaling. We evaluate the method over ten aligned seeds against standard, leaky, deep, orthogonal, cycle, and next-generation reservoirs, together with a compact trained gated recurrent unit, across linear memory, nonlinear recurrence, chaotic forecasting, delayed logic, and real sensor calibration. Across the benchmark suite, the proposed reservoir achieves the best fixed-reservoir performance on bounded NARMA-20 and the lowest mean error on Lorenz-63, matches the strongest linear-memory result, and remains broadly competitive across broad range of benchmarks. Ablation studies show that rotation increases state diversity, whereas dissipation provides controlled forgetting and improves predictive conditioning. The resulting framework offers an interpretable recurrent architecture in which mixing, memory, and stability are explicit and independently tunable design variables.

</details>


### [30] [An Explainable LLM Agent Layer for Open-World Anomaly Detection in Oil Wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04041)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,Thales Miranda de Almeida Vieira,Eduardo Toledo de Lima Junior,William Wagner Matos Lira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在OWL（开放世界学习）油井异常检测流水线下游部署一个LLM智能体层，使用Qwen3.5-397B MoE模型通过NVIDIA NIM服务，接收上游的结构化传感器指标和分类/新颖性断言，返回自然语言合理解释、置信度排序的批评以及为检测到的新颖性事件提供统一的命名。在3W数据集989个真实井段片段上，该智能体在九类分类中达到35.1% top-1 / 63.9% top-3准确率，在7个探测类上达到71.7% top-2验证精度（precision 0.91），新颖性检测达到89.7%且5/7隐藏类具有稳定命名。该智能体旨在弥合OWL流水线在运维场景中部署的可解释性鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OWL油井异常检测流水线虽然能回答"发生了什么"（what happened），但无法解释"模型为何如此认为"（why）以及"操作员下一步应做什么"（what to do next），且检测到的新颖性聚类没有人类可读的命名标签。这一可解释性缺口阻碍了OWL流水线在真实运维环境中的部署。

Method: 在OWL流水线下游放置一个LLM智能体层，作为上游已有方法的"伙伴"而非替代品。智能体使用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts模型（通过NVIDIA NIM部署），接收结构化传感器指标及上游的分类或新颖性断言，输出自然语言解释、按置信度排序的批评意见，并为检测到的新颖性事件提供统一的命名。智能体的四个核心功能：(1) 当传感器证据支持时确认上游决策；(2) 用传感器依据的语言提供可审计的决策解释；(3) 当上游标签不合理时标记分歧；(4) 为新发现的事件命名以提供人类可读的标签。

Result: 在3W数据集上三项研究（989个真实井段片段）：九类分类达到35.1% top-1 / 63.9% top-3（95% CI [56.9, 70.4]）；7个探测类上达到71.7% top-2验证（[64.8, 77.6]），precision 0.91（[0.84, 0.95]）；新颖性检测达到89.7%（[87.0, 91.9]），且5/7隐藏类具有稳定的聚类命名。

Conclusion: LLM智能体不是独立的分类器，其定位是OWL流水线可解释性的关键补充层。通过确认上游决策、提供传感器依据的解释、标记分歧以及为新奇事件命名，该方案弥合了OWL流水线在运维部署中面临的可解释性鸿沟，使操作员能够审计和信任系统输出。

Abstract: Open-World Learning (OWL) pipelines for oil well anomaly detection have recently been shown to combine autoencoder-based detection, multiclass classification, and Mahalanobis-based novelty detection on the public 3W dataset. These pipelines answer \textit{what happened}, but they do not explain \textit{why the model believes it} or \textit{what the operator should do next}, and they do not put a human-readable name on the novelty clusters they discover. This paper evaluates a Large Language Model (LLM) agent layer placed downstream of the OWL pipeline, designed as a \textbf{companion} to the published upstream methods rather than a replacement. Using the Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts model served via NVIDIA NIM, the agent receives structured sensor metrics and upstream classification or novelty assertions, and returns natural-language justifications, confidence-ranked critiques, and consolidated names for detected novelties. Across three studies spanning 989 real well-file segments from the 3W dataset, the agent achieved $35.1\%$ top-1 / $63.9\%$ top-3 (95\% CI [56.9, 70.4]) classification on all nine classes, $71.7\%$ top-2 validation [64.8, 77.6] with precision $0.91$ [0.84, 0.95] across 7 probed classes, and $89.7\%$ novelty detection [87.0, 91.9] with stable cluster naming on 5 of 7 hidden classes. The agent is not a standalone classifier. Its role is to: (1) confirm upstream decisions when sensor evidence supports them, (2) justify decisions in sensor-grounded language operators can audit, (3) flag disagreement when upstream labels are implausible, and (4) name novelties so that clustered unlabeled events arrive at the engineer with a consolidated human-readable label. The goal is to close the explainability gap that currently blocks deployment of OWL pipelines in operational settings.

</details>

*Han Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究梯度下降中参数向量与最大间隔方向的早期弱对齐现象。理论证明在O(exp(exp(-δ)))次迭代内（δ为允许的对齐误差），参数向量即可与最大间隔方向弱对齐，该界被证明是紧的。通过直接追踪径向和切向流，避免渐近展开，建立了更快的弱对齐结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大间隔收敛是一个渐近现象，且渐近收敛速度比纯凸优化慢得多。然而实际中参数向量在远少于渐近迭代次数内就已与最大间隔方向正相关（尽管不完全精确）。作者希望理解这种早期对齐现象的机制，以解释"训练越久，泛化越好"的经验观察。

Method: 通过分别追踪径向（radial）和切向（tangential）流，直接作用于对齐动力学，结合数据集几何信息，绕过了传统渐近展开的方法。建立了参数向量在O(exp(exp(-δ)))次迭代内与最大间隔方向弱对齐的紧界。

Result: 证明了参数向量在O(exp(exp(-δ)))次迭代内与最大间隔方向弱对齐，其中δ>0为允许的对齐误差，且该迭代复杂度上界被证明是紧的。

Conclusion: 早期弱对齐现象可以在极短的迭代次数内发生，这解释了实践中梯度下降在很少轮次内就表现出与最大间隔方向正相关性的经验现象。该工作的关键洞见是摆脱渐近展开，直接从数据集几何出发追踪对齐动力学，从而获得更快的弱对齐速率。

Abstract: Gradient descent has been of particular interest in modern machine learning beyond sole focus on optimization. Implicit bias emerging from optimization, though not being encoded by the learning objective, often prevents from overfitting to spurious patterns. A typical instance is the max-margin implicit bias of a linear classifier, widely established for exponentially tailed loss functions. Even after having a given dataset separated, the parameter vector continues to evolve towards the max-margin direction asymptotically along the gradient descent dynamics. This phenomenon corroborates a frequent empirical observation of "train longer, generalize better." However, the max-margin convergence is an asymptotic phenomenon, and what is worse, this asymptotic convergence rate is significantly slower than pure convex optimization. Even so, the parameter vector along gradient descent dynamics commonly correlates with the max-margin direction positively (though not exactly) within considerably fewer iterations than the asymptotic rate. By shedding another light on this classical problem, this work aims to understand the mechanism of this early-stage alignment phenomenon. Our theoretical results demonstrate that the parameter vector weakly aligns with the max-margin direction within $O(\exp(\exp(-δ)))$ iterations, where $δ>0$ is the permissible alignment error, which is shown to be tight. By tracking the radial and tangential flows, our proof operates on the alignment dynamics directly with dataset geometry and gets rid of the asymptotic expansion, which is a key insight to establishing faster weak alignment.

</details>


### [32] [Tactus: Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition from Low-Cost Pressure Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04043)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tactus是一个基于电阻式压力阵列的开放模型，仅从压力数据即可回答文本查询。在STAG基准上，它用187条训练记录达到0.771±0.062的top-1准确率（top-3为0.935），匹配甚至超越了监督式闭集CNN（0.76），且无需训练分类头。其关键配方是小数据+掩码自编码器预训练（144k无标注帧）+传感器校准仿射。模型误差集中在少数接触模糊类别，与文本目标几何无关，对改写和裸名查询鲁棒，两帧即可恢复89%的八帧精度。模型权重、代码和内存层均开放发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉表征学习长期集中于光学传感器（对变形的凝胶成像），而电阻式压力阵列是最廉价、出货量最大的触觉传感器，却缺乏同样水平的表征学习方法。作者旨在为电阻式压力阵列构建一个能直接从压力数据回答文本查询的开放模型，填补这一空白。

Method: 采用小数据策略：仅用187条训练记录；通过掩码自编码器在144k无标注的同传感器帧上进行预训练；利用传感器自身的校准仿射变换作为关键预处理（该步骤带来的精度提升超过所有架构改动之和）。模型无需训练分类头，直接以文本查询为目标进行推理。

Result: 在STAG基准（27个物体、留出记录）上，四轮运行top-1达到0.771±0.062（top-3为0.935），匹配且有时超出监督式闭集CNN（0.76）。误差集中于少数接触模糊类别，与文本目标几何无相关性（Spearman rho≤0.05，702个类别对），对改写和裸名查询均保持鲁棒，两帧即可恢复89%的八帧精度。同时如实报告失败：跨传感器预训练合并无增益、视觉协同训练反而降低触觉性能、归一化管道错误导致97%动态范围被静默丢弃却产生看似合理的结果。

Conclusion: Tactus证明了仅靠压力数据即可实现与监督式闭集基线相当甚至更优的文本查询应答能力，同时以透明的方式公开了成功与失败细节。该模型、代码及其连接的内存层均公开可用，为触觉表征学习提供了一个基于廉价电阻式阵列的开放替代方案。

Abstract: Resistive pressure arrays are the cheapest and most widely shipped tactile sensors, yet tactile representation learning has concentrated on optical sensors that image a deforming gel. We present Tactus, an open model that answers text queries from pressure data alone: on the STAG benchmark (27 objects, held-out recordings), it reaches 0.771 +/- 0.062 top-1 over four runs (top-3 0.935), matching, and at best exceeding, the dataset's supervised closed-set CNN at 0.76, with no trained classifier head. The recipe is small-data: 187 training recordings, masked-autoencoder pretraining on 144k unlabeled same-sensor frames, and the sensor's own calibration affine, which recovered more accuracy than every architecture change combined. The released model's errors concentrate in a few contact-ambiguous classes, are uncorrelated with text-target geometry (Spearman rho <= 0.05 over 702 class pairs), and survive paraphrased and even bare-name queries within one point; two diverse frames recover 89% of eight-frame accuracy. Failures are reported with equal precision: cross-sensor pretraining pooling gave no gain, vision co-training degraded touch, and a mis-normalized input pipeline silently discarded 97% of the sensor's dynamic range while producing plausible intermediate results. Weights, code, and the memory layer the model plugs into are released openly.

</details>


### [33] [Robust and Personalized Federated Learning for Aircraft-Engine Prognostics under Benign and Adversarial Client Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04045)
*Chinmoy Mitra,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在航空发动机剩余使用寿命预测的联邦学习场景中，系统评估了良性异构性（不同运营条件/故障模式）与恶意异构性（投毒攻击）的联合影响，发现共享表征个性化可弥合约70%的本地与集中式误差差距，而物理驱动的传感器值后门攻击对标准平均聚合器达94.9%成功率，仅Krum聚合器能抵御协同攻击，将个性化与鲁棒聚合结合可在极小精度代价下将攻击成功率降至2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许多个飞机运营商在不共享原始数据的条件下联合训练剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测模型，但面临两类本质不同的挑战：良性异构性（诚实运营商面临不同运营条件和故障模式）与恶意异构性（受攻击运营商提交投毒更新）。现有研究未将两者统一考虑，且需要验证仅凭模型精度能否保证安全。

Method: 使用多任务一维卷积神经网络，基于C-MAPSS基准的结构性非独立同分布数据划分，对比四种良性异构性缓解方法（共享表征个性化、近端正则化、服务端重加权等），并设计五种攻击（含物理动机的传感器值后门，用于掩盖发动机退化）对四种聚合方法进行安全评估。

Result: 共享表征个性化弥合约70%的本地与集中式RMSE差距（近端正则化21%、服务端重加权仅10%）；物理后门攻击对标准平均聚合取得94.9%成功率且不显著影响干净精度；Krum使攻击成功率降低一个数量级，是唯一抵御协同攻击的聚合器；个性化+鲁棒聚合组合将攻击成功率降至2.8%且精度损失很小。结果在客户数量和更难的六条件数据集上保持一致。

Conclusion: 精度指标本身不足以证明模型安全性，攻击成功率必须被显式评估；个性化与鲁棒聚合存在互补性，需在鲁棒更新选择与协作表征学习之间权衡，二者结合是最佳策略。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables aircraft fleet operators to jointly train remaining-useful-life (RUL) models from engine sensor telemetry without sharing raw data. This study examines two complementary challenges: benign heterogeneity, where honest operators observe different operating conditions and fault modes, and adversarial heterogeneity, where compromised operators submit poisoned updates. We conduct a controlled, safety-oriented evaluation using a multi-task one-dimensional convolutional neural network and a structurally non-IID partition of the Commercial Modular Aero-Propulsion System Simulation (C-MAPSS) benchmark. We compare four remedies for benign heterogeneity and evaluate five attacks against four aggregation methods, including a physically motivated sensor-value backdoor designed to mask engine degradation.
  Shared-representation personalization closes approximately 70% of the local-to-centralized root-mean-square-error gap, compared with 21% for proximal regularization and 10% for server-side reweighting. The backdoor achieves a 94.9% attack success rate against standard averaging while leaving clean accuracy statistically unchanged, demonstrating that accuracy alone cannot certify model safety and that attack success must be evaluated explicitly. Krum reduces attack success by an order of magnitude and is the only evaluated aggregator that withstands coordinated attackers, whereas personalization alone provides no protection. Combining personalization with robust aggregation restores robustness (2.8% attack success) with only a small accuracy cost, revealing a trade-off between robust update selection and collaborative representation learning. Results remain consistent across client counts and on a harder six-condition dataset. Code and data partitions are released for reproducibility.

</details>

*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究函数分布上的流匹配（flow matching）方法在仅有有限个系数或点值实现时的收敛性理论。作者证明了对于任意强一致的有限秩重构序列，有限条件速度目标具有强L²收敛性，并给出了定量界；对于学习流，通过直接耦合总体叠加路径得到端到端Wasserstein界，无需假设总体有限维ODE的唯一性。此外，论文通过归一化求积神经算子验证了传感器无关常数，并给出了多个技术性论证（空间正则性-求积证书、Bernstein论证、裁剪高斯缩放的显式端到端速率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数分布上的流匹配本质上是定义在函数分布上的，但在实际实现中只能从有限系数或点值出发。当使用散乱或自适应细化时，对应的条件sigma-代数不一定嵌套，因此经典鞅收敛理论无法保证传感器极限的收敛性。这构成了理论验证的空白，需要建立新的收敛性框架。

Method: (1) 对任意强一致的有限秩重构序列，证明有限条件速度目标在L²意义下的强收敛，其中正交投影情形给出定量界，点传感器情形通过正则性空间扩展；(2) 学习流中直接与总体叠加路径耦合，获得端到端Wasserstein界而不依赖总体有限维ODE的唯一性；(3) 验证归一化求积神经算子的传感器无关常数，包括通过显式幅值递推处理全局Lipschitz激活；(4) 通过非交换迹类高斯示例对比投影限制与精确条件下的边界乘子；(5) 利用空间正则性-求积证书闭合算子实现项，Bernstein论证给出固定模型维数与包络下的O~(n^{-1})超额风险项，裁剪高斯缩放特例给出显式端到端速率。

Result: (1) 建立了强L²收敛性并给出定量收敛界；(2) 获得端到端Wasserstein上界，且不依赖总体有限维ODE唯一性假设；(3) 非交换高斯示例显示投影限制下边界乘子为0，精确条件下为0.72；(4) 超额风险项为O~(n^{-1})；(5) 裁剪高斯缩放特例得到显式端到端收敛速率。

Conclusion: 论文建立了函数化流匹配在有限传感器实现下的严格收敛性理论框架，解决了散乱/自适应细化中sigma-代数不嵌套导致的鞅收敛失效问题，并通过定量界、数值示例和显式速率全面验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: Functional flow matching is posed on distributions of functions but implemented from finitely many coefficients or point values. Under scattered or adaptive refinement, the resulting conditioning sigma-algebras need not be nested, so martingale convergence does not justify the sensor limit. We prove strong $L^2$ convergence of finite conditional velocity targets for every strongly consistent sequence of finite-rank reconstructions, with quantitative bounds for orthogonal projections and a point-sensor extension through a regularity space. For learned flows, coupling directly to a population superposition path yields an end-to-end Wasserstein bound without assuming uniqueness of the population finite-dimensional ODE. We verify sensor-independent constants for a normalized quadrature neural operator, including globally Lipschitz activations through an explicit magnitude recurrence. A noncommuting trace-class Gaussian example gives boundary multiplier $0$ under projected restriction and $0.72$ under exact conditioning. A spatial regularity--cubature certificate closes the operator-realization term, a Bernstein argument gives a $\widetilde{O}(n^{-1})$ excess-risk term for fixed model dimension and envelopes, and an exactly realizable clipped Gaussian scaling specialization yields an explicit end-to-end rate.

</details>


### [35] [Recurrent Residual Quantization: A Progressive Multi-Precision Representation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04048)
*Yu Luo,Bo Dong,Wenhua Cheng,Haihao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归残差量化（RRQ），一种后训练量化（PTQ）框架，通过从2-bit基础模型逐步添加轻量级2-bit残差来构建4、6、8位表示，实现从单一检查点获得多种有效精度，无需校准且避免联合多位优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不同部署约束下服务LLM需要灵活性，即在精度、内存占用和吞吐量之间进行权衡。传统量化方法每个目标位宽需要单独的检查点，缺乏灵活性和效率。

Method: RRQ将权重表示为低bit量化基加上一系列量化残差修正。从通过PTQ或RTN获得的2-bit模型出发，逐步添加由RTN生成的轻量级2-bit残差，以构建4、6、8位表示。该方法无需校准，且避免了联合多位优化。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，完整的all-RTN 2/4/6/8位包在1,293秒内构建完成，比MatGPTQ快3.3倍。在六个近期LLM上的实验显示，6位和8位精度具有竞争力，4位时行为依赖于具体模型。

Conclusion: RRQ提供了一种高效、灵活的量化方案，允许从单一检查点出发在不同精度之间灵活切换，适用于多样化的部署需求。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under diverse deployment constraints requires flexible trade-offs between accuracy, memory footprint, and throughput. However, conventional quantization methods typically require a separate checkpoint for each target bit-width. We introduce Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ), a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that represents weights as a low-bit quantized base together with a sequence of quantized residual corrections, enabling multiple effective precisions from a single checkpoint. Starting from a 2-bit model obtained via post-training quantization (PTQ) or round-to-nearest (RTN), RRQ progressively adds lightweight 2-bit residuals generated via RTN to construct 4-, 6-, and 8-bit representations. The method is calibration-free and avoids joint multi-bit optimization. In our Qwen3-8B setup, the full all-RTN 2-/4-/6-/8-bit package is constructed in 1,293 seconds, 3.3 times faster than the measured MatGPTQ construction. Experiments on six recent LLMs show competitive accuracy at 6 and 8 bits, with model-dependent behavior at 4 bits. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [36] [An entropic explanation of insistence on sameness in autism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04616)
*Przemysław Śliwiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于信息论的框架，将自闭症中的"坚持同一性"（insistence on sameness）解释为个体减少惊讶和不确定性的一般行为模式。文章将自闭症重新定义为认知功能局限于对环境有形属性的辨别、记忆和预测的损伤，并通过D_H(R,M)熵度量公式论证坚持同一性是限制刺激以匹配已知记忆的熵最小化行为。该框架可量化惊讶、不确定性、感官过载等概念，为学习疗法和日常护理提供优化算法指导，并可通过类图灵测试方式验证，无需涉及自闭症患者的实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对自闭症"坚持同一性"现象的理解缺乏统一的形式化理论框架。作者试图从信息论角度解释这一行为，将其纳入更普遍的减少惊讶和不确定性的行为模式，并提供可量化、可计算的理论基础，从而为自闭症治疗和护理提供系统性的形式化指导。

Method: 作者构建了信息论框架，定义了熵度量D_H(R,M) = H(R|M) + H(M|R)，其中R为随机刺激序列，M为存储和检索它们的记忆。通过分析该度量最小化的两种途径——学习R（将知识存入M）或限制R使仅出现已知内容——论证自闭症患者倾向于采取后者，即坚持同一性。此外提出了对框架的验证方法（类图灵测试方式）以及将疗法转化为优化算法的实施方案。

Result: 研究表明：坚持同一性是熵最小化的一种表现方式；该框架能够量化惊讶、不确定性、感官过载与剥夺、焦虑、舒适区、失望、迷失方向、迂腐、僵化、苛守或异常精确性等概念；可生成学习疗法和日常护理的指导清单并将其定义为优化算法，可编程用于机器人护理照护者；可通过不需要自闭症个体参与的类图灵测试方案进行框架验证。

Conclusion: 该信息论框架为理解自闭症中的坚持同一性行为提供了形式化的理论基础。若经正向验证，将为改善自闭症个体在基本日常活动中的自理能力的疗法提供正式基础和设计指南。

Abstract: An information theory-based framework is proposed in attempt to explain insistence on sameness in autism as an instance of a general behavior pattern in which an individual tries to reduce surprise and uncertainty. It offers a new definition of autism as an impairment in which cognitive functions are restricted to discrimination, memorization and prediction of tangible properties of the environment. An analogy between insistence on sameness and constrained minimization of the entropy metric is observed and examined for a set of assumptions that describe cognitive limitations of a person with autism. The metric is given by the formula $D_H(R, M) = H(R|M) + H(M|R)$, where $R$ represents sequences of random stimuli, $M$ is a memory that stores and retrieves them, and where $H(.|.)$ denotes their conditional entropies interpreted as surprise and uncertainty, respectively. It is first inferred that to minimize the metric an individual can learn about $R$ (and store that knowledge in $M$) or can restrict $R$ to the already known $M$. Then, it is concluded that insistence on sameness is a manifestation of the latter. Moreover, it is shown that the proposed framework: (1) Helps to quantify the concepts of surprise, uncertainty, sensory overload and deprivation, anxiety, comfort zone, disappointment, disorientation, pedantry, rigidness, observance or aberrant precision. (2) Leads to a list of guidelines for learning therapies and daily care routines, and allows them to be defined as optimization algorithms and implemented as programs for robotic live-in caregivers. (3) Can be validated with the help of a Turing test-like approach that requires no experiments involving individuals with autism. The framework-if positively validated-will provide formal foundations and design guidelines for therapies aimed at improving self-reliance of individuals with autism in basic activities of daily living.

</details>


### [37] [CAMP: A Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04051)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMP是一个面向长期时间序列预测的周期感知多尺度Patch混合模型，通过自适应周期学习、视界引导的Patch混合和去周期残差的多尺度建模，在七个长期预测基准和四个PEMS交通基准上取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列的周期模式往往随数据集、预测设置和输入窗口而变化，现有周期感知预测器通常仅在数据集层面选择一个固定周期，难以处理周期随时间变化或多周期并存的情况。此外，基于Patch的模型对所有Patch位置统一处理，忽略了远离预测边界的位置需要更广泛的上下文信息，而靠近边界的位置应更直接地保留信息。去除周期后的残差动力学也跨越多个时间分辨率，单尺度描述不足。

Method: CAMP包含三个核心组件：1) Adaptive Cycle Learning模块，为每个输入窗口单独识别主导频率并生成历史和未来周期分量，无需预设周期长度；2) Horizon-Guided Patch Mixer，引入位置相关精炼机制，让较早的Patch融入更广泛的时间上下文，同时保留靠近预测边界的信息；3) 对去周期残差进行时间对齐的多分辨率表示建模，在单一预测框架内捕获不同尺度的互补动力学。

Result: 在七个长期预测基准上，CAMP在六个数据集上取得最佳平均MSE，在六个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳MAE；在四个PEMS交通基准的十六个设置中取得了最高的MSE获胜次数。

Conclusion: CAMP通过自适应周期学习、视界引导的Patch混合和多尺度残差建模，有效解决了固定周期假设、统一Patch处理和单尺度残差建模的局限性，在多个长期时间序列预测基准上展现了优越性能。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often governed by recurring patterns, but their dominant periods may vary across datasets, forecasting settings, and individual input windows. Existing cycle-aware forecasters commonly rely on a single period selected at the dataset level, which can be restrictive when periodic behavior changes over time or when multiple cycles coexist. Moreover, patch-based models typically process all patch positions uni- formly, although patches farther from the forecast boundary may require broader contextual refinement, while recent patches contain information that should be preserved more directly. Af- ter cyclic behavior is removed, the remaining dynamics may also span multiple temporal resolutions and cannot be adequately de- scribed at a single scale. We introduce CAMP, a Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer designed to address these challenges. The Adaptive Cycle Learning module identifies dominant fre- quencies separately for each input window and generates both historical and future cyclic components without requiring a pre- defined cycle length. The Horizon-Guided Patch Mixer intro- duces position-dependent refinement, allowing earlier patches to incorporate broader temporal context while preserving infor- mation close to the forecast boundary. CAMP further models the de-cycled residual through temporally aligned multi-resolution representations, enabling complementary dynamics at different scales to be captured within one forecasting framework. Across seven long-term forecasting benchmarks, CAMP achieves the best average MSE on six datasets and the best or tied-best MAE on six. It also obtains the highest MSE win count across sixteen settings on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>

*Priyanka Nihalchandani,Naman Srivastava,Varun Ojha,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于时间序列基础模型（TSFM）在建筑能耗预测中的个性化联邦稀疏适配框架，采用异构时间混合专家（MoE）适配器，通过序列级路由器将每个168小时上下文窗口映射到专门的专家子集。实验表明，个性化策略在50栋建筑和三个TSFM骨干上始终优于全局FL-MoE和局部MoE，且最佳稀疏适配策略因骨干和指标而异。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能耗预测中，仪表数据是私有、分布式且高度非IID的，联邦适配TSFM具有吸引力。然而单一参数共享策略难以适配所有预训练TSFM或建筑客户端：完全共享适配器会抑制建筑特定的时间行为，而完全局部适配则丢弃了跨建筑的知识迁移。

Method: 提出个性化联邦稀疏适配框架，在预训练TSFM表示后放置异构时间MoE适配器。采用序列级路由器将每个168小时上下文窗口映射到top-k个专家子集，专家分别专精于周期性、长程交互、局部变化、趋势残差结构和多分辨率行为。对比了全局FL、局部训练与个性化FL变体，以及全局共享或客户端私有专家库。

Result: 在50栋建筑和三个TSFM骨干上，个性化方法始终优于全局FL-MoE和局部MoE。最佳稀疏适配策略随骨干和评估指标而变化。路由行为揭示了客户端级专家专业化、专家集中度以及跨骨干的近均匀路由模式。

Conclusion: 联邦TSFM适配应同时考虑客户端感知和骨干感知，单一适配策略无法通用，需要根据建筑特征和预训练骨干特性进行个性化设计。

Abstract: Federated adaptation of time-series foundation models (TSFMs) is attractive for building energy forecasting because meter data are private, distributed, and highly non-IID. However, a single parameter-sharing strategy is unlikely to serve all pretrained TSFMs or building clients: fully shared adapters can suppress building-specific temporal behavior, while fully local adaptation discards cross-building transfer. We propose a personalized federated sparse adaptation framework with a heterogeneous temporal mixture-of-experts (MoE) adapter placed after the pretrained TSFM representation. A sequence-level router maps each 168-hour context window to a top-$k$ subset of experts specialized for periodicity, long-range interactions, local variation, trend-residual structure, and multi-resolution behavior. We compare global FL, local training, and personalized FL variants with globally shared or client-private expert banks. Across 50 buildings and three TSFM backbones, personalization consistently outperforms Global FL-MoE and Local MoE, while the best sparse-adaptation strategy varies by backbone and metric. Routing behavior further reveals client-level expert specialization, expert concentration, and near-uniform routing across backbones, showing that federated TSFM adaptation should be both client-aware and backbone-aware.

</details>


### [39] [LaPrune: Controllable Differentiable Sparsity at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04057)
*Jakub Antczak,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LaPrune，一种具有精确预算的可微分稀疏掩码层，通过LapSum屏障保持选择质量，并通过归一化二阶矩约束将掩码从密集等质量分配推向硬top-k选择，同时推导出饱和分数的总体预测、近二值极限定律和近零分数的紧最坏情况保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏模型中的top-k选择是确定哪些组件保持活跃的关键。硬选择会阻断梯度，而连续松弛方法通常将掩码的硬度与选择质量耦合在一起，导致无法独立控制硬度和预算。需要一种既能保持精确预算又能提供可控硬度的方法。

Method: 提出LaPrune层：使用LapSum屏障保持选择质量（精确预算），并通过归一化二阶矩约束将掩码从密集等质量分配演化为硬top-k分配。推导了饱和分数的人口预测、近二值极限定律以及近零分数的最紧最坏情况保证。归一化硬度参数对分数尺度具有不变性，而固定LapSum温度则不具备此性质。

Result: LaPrune在不同预算下能够将掩码从密集分配平滑地推向硬top-k，其归一化硬度参数对分数尺度不变；提供了饱和分数的解析预测和近零分数的最紧最坏情况保证。

Conclusion: LaPrune作为数学上精确预算的可微分层，能够在控制归一化二阶矩的同时保持选择质量，解决了硬选择梯度阻断与松弛方法中硬度-预算耦合的问题。

Abstract: Top-$k$ selection determines which components of a sparse model remain active. Hard selection blocks gradients, while continuous relaxations often couple mask hardness to the selected mass. We introduce LaPrune, a mathematically exact-budget differentiable layer that controls the normalized second moment while preserving the selected mass. A LapSum barrier preserves the selection mass, and a normalized second-moment constraint moves the mask from a dense equal-mass allocation toward hard top-$k$ at each budget. We derive a population prediction of the saturated fraction, a near-binary limiting law, and a tight worst-case guarantee on the near-zero fraction. The normalized hardness parameter is invariant to score scale, while a fixed LapSum temperature is not.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为有限温度下训练于高斯混合的连续自旋感知机提供严格的变分分析框架，填补该模型在理论求解上的空白。

Method: 结合插值法、对数凹性与集中不等式，推导出淬火压力的下界和上界极小极大变分界限；利用变分势能的凹-凸结构控制极值，并分析两个变分参数优化的可交换性。

Result: 上下界仅在两个变分参数的优化顺序上不同；当两个优化可交换时，上下界匹配并解出模型；该势能同时给出不动点方程作为平稳性条件，并统一计算基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差。

Conclusion: 该变分框架为有限温度连续自旋感知机提供了统一的理论求解路径，在参数优化可交换的条件下可获得精确解。

Abstract: We introduce a variational approach to a finite-temperature continuous-spin perceptron trained on a Gaussian mixture. The model allows for a broad class of concave utilities and log-concave separable prior measures on the spins. By combining the interpolation method with log-concavity and concentration estimates, we derive lower and upper minimax variational bounds for the limiting quenched pressure. Remarkably, the two bounds differ only in the order of optimization of two variational parameters, while all remaining extrema are controlled by the concave--convex structure of the variational potential. Whenever the two optimizations commute, the two bounds match and identify the solution of the model. The same potential yields the fixed-point equations as stationarity conditions and provides a unified route to the computation of the ground-state energy, training loss, and generalization error.

</details>


### [41] [SJEPA: Learning Elegant Latent Dynamics with Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04060)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SJEPA框架，一种无重建的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），其学习到的预测表示能诱导出紧凑的符号化动力学描述。混合转移模型结合符号定律与正则化神经修正，核心原则是学习最简单的充分动力学，实验证明联合学习相比事后拟合能发现更简单的符号动力学，且能可控权衡预测保真度、表示质量、符号简洁性与符号-神经分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统JEPA架构中，转移模型通常是不可解释的神经映射，其学习到的抽象表示虽然能预测目标嵌入，但诱导的动力学是黑箱的。作者希望突破这一局限，让预测表示的转移动力学能够产生紧凑、可解释的符号描述，从而在保持JEPA预测能力的同时获得可解释性。

Method: SJEPA采用混合转移模型：一个符号定律负责主导动力学，一个正则化的神经修正处理所选语法之外的残差动力学。其核心原则体现在两个层面：(1) 表示约束：保留信息丰富、非坍缩的预测坐标；(2) 算子压缩：偏好低复杂度的符号-神经转移。作者形式化了诱导动力学复杂度，分析了预测坐标的不可辨识性，并证明无约束的算子压缩会直接导致表示坍缩的捷径。框架支持交替的表示-方程学习以及符号动力学拟合固定表示两种模式。

Result: 在受控摆锤实验中，联合学习相比事后拟合能发现显著更简单的符号动力学，且具有更低的长期展开误差和散度；无约束的单步诊断确实实现了预测的坍缩捷径。在语法错误设定（grammar misspecification）下，修正正则化保留了可表示的符号机制，并将神经组件导向残差动力学。结果展示了预测保真度、表示质量、符号简洁性与符号-神经分配之间可控的权衡。

Conclusion: SJEPA证明了通过联合优化方法，可以在JEPA框架内获得可解释的符号化转移动力学，同时避免表示坍缩。该方法揭示了一条可控的权衡路径，使研究者可以根据任务需求在预测能力与符号可解释性之间进行调节。该工作为构建兼具预测能力和可解释性的表示学习模型提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn abstract states by predicting target embeddings from context embeddings, but their transition models are typically opaque neural maps. We introduce SJEPA, a reconstruction-free JEPA framework that learns predictive representations whose induced dynamics admit compact symbolic descriptions. Its hybrid transition combines a symbolic law with a regularised neural correction for dynamics outside the selected grammar. The central principle is to learn the simplest adequate dynamics: representation constraints preserve informative, non-collapsed predictive coordinates, while operator compression favours low-complexity symbolic-neural transitions that remain predictively adequate. We formalise this principle through induced-dynamics complexity, analyse predictive-coordinate non-identifiability, and show that unconstrained operator compression creates a direct shortcut to representation collapse. The framework supports both alternating representation-equation learning and symbolic dynamics fitted to fixed representations. In controlled pendulum experiments, joint learning discovers substantially simpler symbolic dynamics with lower long-horizon rollout error and divergence than post-hoc fitting, while an unconstrained one-step diagnostic realises the predicted collapse shortcut. Under grammar misspecification, correction regularisation preserves the representable symbolic mechanism and directs the neural component towards residual dynamics. The results expose a controllable trade-off among predictive fidelity, representation quality, symbolic parsimony, and symbolic-neural allocation.

</details>


### [42] [Stochastic Emulation using Generalized Stratified Sampling for Performance-Based Risk Optimization of Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05006)
*Isabela D. Rodrigues,Seymour M. J. Spence,Henrique M. Kroetz,André T. Beck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将广义分层抽样（GSS）与随机多项式混沌展开（SPCE）相结合的框架（GSS-SPCE），用于基于性能的风险优化（PBRO）中结构在随机荷载下的概率约束评估。该框架通过按灾害强度对输入空间分层并分别训练SPCE仿真器，改善了对结构响应尾部极端值的表示能力，并在不显著增加非线性模型评估次数的前提下提高了概率约束估计的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPCE虽在PBRO中避免了重复非线性分析，但在准确表示结构响应分布尾部极端值方面存在局限性，这可能导致概率约束的评估出现偏差。

Method: 将GSS方案与SPCE结合：GSS根据灾害强度将输入空间划分为多个层，在每个层内独立训练SPCE仿真器，利用全概率定理对各层估计的条件超越概率进行重组，以评估概率约束。

Result: 在两层钢框架建筑的屈曲约束支撑截面优化设计中，GSS-SPCE框架准确估计了结构响应分布（包括尾部区域），同时大幅减少了PBRO所需的非线性模型评估次数。

Conclusion: GSS-SPCE框架有效克服了SPCE在尾部分布表示上的不足，实现了准确性与计算效率之间的良好平衡，可用于实际工程结构在随机荷载下的基于性能的风险优化设计。

Abstract: Metamodels are instrumental in reducing the computational burden associated with nested reliability analyses and optimization loops in Performance-Based Risk Optimization (PBRO) of structures under stochastic loads. In this context, stochastic emulators are particularly useful because they approximate response distributions while accounting for the intrinsic stochasticity of the simulator. Among these methods, Stochastic Polynomial Chaos Expansion (SPCE) is especially attractive because it does not require replications of nonlinear analyses at fixed input conditions. However, SPCE may present limitations in accurately representing extreme responses in the tails of structural response distributions. To address this limitation, this study proposes a framework that combines Generalized Stratified Sampling (GSS) with SPCE. The GSS scheme partitions the input space into strata according to the intensity of the hazard, improving the representation of extreme responses, while independent SPCE emulators are trained within each stratum. The conditional exceedance probabilities estimated in each stratum are then recombined using the total probability theorem to evaluate the probabilistic constraints. The proposed GSS-SPCE framework is applied to the optimal design of buckling-restrained brace cross-sectional areas in a two-story steel building. The objective is to minimize the initial construction cost while satisfying prescribed probabilistic performance constraints. Results show that the proposed framework accurately estimates structural response distributions, including their tail regions, while substantially reducing the number of nonlinear model evaluations required for PBRO.

</details>


### [43] [Spend Bits Where Queries Look: KV Cache Vector Quantization with Attention-Preserving Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04074)
*Samuel Fernández-Menduiña,Amir Ziashahabi,Eduardo Pavez,Antonio Ortega,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NOVA-KV，一种基于变换编码的KV缓存量化方法。通过从校准统计中推导闭式最优变换（非正交，满足广义Parseval关系），将注意力感知失真转化为变换域的MSE，并使用MSE最优的矢量量化器。在每元素2比特下，NOVA-KV显著恢复了长上下文检索精度，同时保持可比吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码每一步都需读取KV缓存，其加载时间超过注意力计算时间，导致吞吐量受带宽限制。减少缓存大小可同时提升解码速度和部署容量。现有方法要么是数据无关的，要么未从失真准则推导变换；且依赖随机或Hadamard旋转（仅均衡方差而非压缩能量）和固定宽度标量量化器（低码率下次优）。

Method: 将KV缓存量化建模为变换编码问题，失真定义为注意力乘积误差。在高分辨率模型下，从校准统计中推导键和值的闭式最优变换。证明最优键变换非正交，满足广义Parseval关系，使注意力感知失真等价于变换域的MSE，从而可直接使用MSE最优的矢量量化器。为满足固定位宽布局，将系数分组到等体积划分，使等大小码本达到变速率最优。

Result: 在每元素2比特下，NOVA-KV恢复了大部分由标量量化方法在长上下文检索任务中损失的精度，同时保持相当的吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过将KV量化重新定义为变换编码问题并推导注意力感知的最优变换，NOVA-KV在低比特率下实现了优于现有方法（如随机/Hadamard旋转+标量量化）的精度-吞吐量权衡，为KV缓存压缩提供了理论基础更强的方案。

Abstract: Long-context LLM decoding reads the key-value (KV) cache at every step. Loading it takes longer than computing attention over it, so throughput is bandwidth-bound. Hence, reducing the cache size can raise both decoding speed and serving capacity. The challenge is to reduce cache size while preserving the attention products, keeping reconstruction cheap, and using a fixed per-token bit count. At two bits per element, the most competitive methods rely on orthogonal transforms. However, existing techniques are either data-oblivious or use the query statistics without deriving the transform from a distortion criterion. Moreover, they rely on transforms built on top of random or Hadamard rotations, which equalize variances across entries rather than compacting energy, and fixed-width scalar quantizers, which are suboptimal at low rates. In this paper, we formulate KV cache quantization as a transform coding problem in which distortion is the error in the attention products. We derive closed-form optimal transforms for keys and values from calibration statistics, under a high-resolution model. We show that the optimal key transform is not orthogonal and satisfies a generalized Parseval relation: the attention-aware distortion becomes mean-squared error (MSE) in the transform domain. Thus, we can use MSE-optimal vector quantizers applied directly to the transformed key coefficients. To meet the fixed-width layout requirement, we show that grouping coefficients into equal-volume partitions makes equal-size codebooks attain the variable-rate optimum under the same high-resolution model. At two bits per element, our method, termed NOVA-KV, recovers most of the long-context retrieval accuracy lost by scalar quantization methods at comparable throughput.

</details>


### [44] [Canonical Joint Energy-Based Model on CIFAR-10: failure modes and practical indistinguishability of Predictor-Corrector and SGLD samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05025)
*Dmytro Knopov*

Main category: cs.LG
