<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练可以提升大语言模型的推理能力，但自回归rollout生成是主要效率瓶颈。投机解码可以加速生成，但在RL过程中直接应用很困难，因为目标策略持续演化：静态proposer会变得过时，而频繁更新drafter则带来额外开销。因此需要一种能随目标策略自适应演化的投机解码方案。

Method: SpecRoll提出了双时间尺度自适应机制：(1) 轻量级future-token heads用于生成并行proposals；(2) Reflex模块利用延迟的verifier反馈进行有界的、轨迹局部的hidden-state修正（无需反向传播）；(3) 慢路径仅在检测到持续性能退化时更新head参数；(4) 结合并发感知的稀疏树验证和精确目标验证，保持目标rollout分布和GRPO目标不变。

Result: 在五个模型（1.5B到14B）和三个数学推理数据集上，SpecRoll相比vanilla GRPO实现1.26-2.15x的生成加速和1.21-2.04x的端到端加速。在所有15个匹配设置中均优于FastGRPO，平均端到端增益1.18x。消融实验显示快慢两条适应路径提供互补性收益。

Conclusion: SpecRoll通过双时间尺度自适应机制有效解决了RL训练中目标策略动态演化对投机解码的挑战，在不改变目标分布的前提下显著提升训练效率，快慢两条适应路径的互补设计是其取得一致性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but autoregressive rollout generation remains a major efficiency bottleneck. Speculative decoding can accelerate generation, yet applying it during RL is difficult because the target policy continually evolves: static proposers become stale, while frequent drafter updates add substantial overhead. We introduce SpecRoll, a speculative rollout engine that preserves the target model's sampling distribution while adapting at two timescales. Lightweight future-token heads generate parallel proposals, while our proposed Reflex module uses delayed verifier feedback to perform bounded, trajectory-local hidden-state corrections without backpropagation. A complementary slow path updates the head parameters only when sustained degradation is detected. SpecRoll combines these mechanisms with concurrency-aware sparse-tree verification and exact target verification, leaving the target rollout distribution and GRPO objective unchanged. Across five models ranging from 1.5B to 14B and three mathematical reasoning datasets, SpecRoll achieves 1.26-2.15x generation speedup and 1.21-2.04x end-to-end speedup over vanilla GRPO. It also outperforms FastGRPO in both generation and end-to-end time across all 15 matched settings, with an average pairwise end-to-end gain of 1.18x. Controlled ablations show that the fast and slow adaptation paths provide complementary benefits. Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SpecRoll-26062006.

</details>


### [111] [EvolveNet: Collaborative Harness Evolution for Agent Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04968)
*Jun Nie,Yonggang Zhang,Qianshu Cai,Yiu-ming Cheung,Xinmei Tian,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvolveNet，一种协作式harness进化范式，将经验提取移动到数据本地，通过广播共享harness并仅聚合各agent的程序适配结果，实现无需集中原始数据的多智能体并行进化，在五个基准设置上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness进化方法假设所有执行经验可以路由到单一优化器，沿顺序轨迹进化单一harness。但真实agent生态中，用户、组织和环境产生隔离的经验流，无法集中共享，而最具学习价值的经验恰恰是无法直接集中化的那部分。这构成了现有方法的根本局限。

Method: EvolveNet采用协作式harness进化范式：将共享harness广播到数据本地的agent部署点，每个部署在其自身工作负载上独立进化harness；仅将产生的程序适配（adaptations）组合为更新后的共享harness并重新分发，使各agent继承彼此的运行经验。核心创新包括将聚合边界从原始工作负载移动到学习到的适配（保持工作负载本地化、支持并发搜索并降低序列深度），以及引入作用域类型化（scope-typed）、证据引导（evidence-guided）的程序聚合方法来处理独立修改程序间的冲突问题。

Result: 在文本到SQL、数据科学编码、竞争性编程、软件工程和agentic工作流五类设置中，EvolveNet在所有五个场景均提升了共享harness，其中异质工作负载下收益最大。消融实验表明改进源于不同agent间适配的组合而非挑选。

Conclusion: EvolveNet验证了数据本地化的协作式harness进化是可行且有效的，通过组合而非选择不同agent的适配可以持续改进共享harness，突破了传统方法对经验集中化的假设，为分布式agent生态中的harness进化提供了新范式。

Abstract: The capabilities of an LLM agent depend not only on its model but on the harness: the executable program that constructs context, invokes tools, verifies results, and recovers from failure. Recent work shows that evolving the harness yields persistent improvements without updating model weights. Existing approaches, however, assume that all execution experience can be routed to a single optimizer, which evolves one harness along a sequential trajectory. Real agent ecosystems violate that assumption: users, organizations, and environments generate isolated streams of experience that cannot be pooled, so the experience most worth learning from is exactly the experience that cannot be directly centralized. We introduce EvolveNet, a paradigm of collaborative harness evolution that moves experience extraction to the data. A shared harness is broadcast to data-local agent deployments, each of which evolves it on its own workload. Only the resulting program adaptations are composed into an updated shared harness and redistributed, so that every participating agent inherits operational experience discovered by the others. By shifting the aggregation boundary from raw workloads to learned adaptations, EvolveNet keeps workloads local and allows multiple evolutionary searches to proceed concurrently with reduced serial depth. Because independently modified programs cannot be averaged like model parameters and may conflict when composed, EvolveNet introduces scope-typed, evidence-guided program aggregation. Across five settings spanning text-to-SQL, data-science coding, competitive programming, software engineering, and agentic workflows, EvolveNet improves the shared harness in all five, with the largest gains under heterogeneous workloads, and ablations attribute the improvement to composition of adaptations from different agents rather than to selecting among them.

</details>


### [112] [Optimizing What Policies Learn From: Recoverability-aware Rollout Intervention Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05080)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Manqing Mao,Hong Wang,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Jie Yuan,Yanjun Lin,James Feng,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Yanfang Ye,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAIL是一个训练时框架，通过在线上下文赌博机建模干预选择，学习在训练过程中如何为策略优化生成更有效的rollout，从而在有限预算下提升大语言模型后训练的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于群体的强化学习方法（如GRPO）在训练时为每个任务和轨迹状态分配相同数量的rollouts，忽视了不同rollout提供学习信号的价值差异。虽然已有工作开始将rollout生成视为自适应决策，但仍存在两个关键局限：一是干预策略基于固定启发式规则，无法随策略变化调整；二是只决策生成多少rollout，没有显式控制在哪里以及如何干预。

Method: RAIL将干预选择建模为在线上下文赌博机问题，训练一个可恢复性控制器（recoverability controller），通过shadow-to-live程序收集干预轨迹来持续学习。该控制器能够在底层策略演化过程中保持适应性学习。

Result: 在多个设置中，RAIL在有限的rollout预算下持续提升了性能，验证了其在效率、适应性、表达力和有效性方面的优势。

Conclusion: 可恢复性感知的干预提供了一种原则化方法来生成更具信息量和更少冗余的rollouts，从而在后训练过程中产生更强的学习信号。

Abstract: Critic-free group-based reinforcement learning has become a scalable approach for post-training large language models. However, most existing methods allocate the same number of rollouts to every task and trajectory state, even though some rollouts provide much more useful learning signals than others. Recent work has started to treat rollout generation as an adaptive decision, but two important limitations remain. First, intervention strategies are often based on fixed heuristics and therefore cannot adjust as the policy changes during training. Second, these methods usually decide only how many rollouts to generate, without explicitly controlling where and how to intervene. To address these limitations, we propose Recoverability-Aware Intervention Learning (RAIL), a training-time framework that learns how to generate rollouts based on the improvement produced by each intervention. RAIL models intervention selection as an online contextual-bandit problem and trains a recoverability controller using intervention traces collected through a shadow-to-live procedure. This allows the controller to keep learning while the underlying policy evolves. We evaluate RAIL in terms of effectiveness, adaptivity, expressiveness, and efficiency. Across multiple settings, RAIL consistently improves performance under limited rollout budgets. These results show that recoverability-aware intervention provides a principled way to generate more informative and less redundant rollouts, leading to stronger learning signals during post-training.

</details>


### [113] [Learning When to Stop: Prefix-Optimal Dynamic Diffusion Policies for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05084)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Manoj Saravanan,Simon Stepputtis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出前缀最优生成策略（POGP），通过类似于Bellman递归的方式在去噪链的每个中间步骤学习前缀值函数，该值函数既作为辅助训练目标提升中间输出的质量，又作为测试时自适应早停的判据。在四个MuJoCo环境和12个基线的比较中，POGP将所需去噪迭代次数减少约2.7倍，同时保持近乎完整的任务性能，并将最终任务性能相对最优动态扩散基线提升约3.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在连续控制中表现出色，但其迭代去噪过程带来了严重的计算瓶颈。为了降低计算成本，需要根据每个动作的难度自适应调整去噪步数，同时保持任务性能不下降。

Method: 提出POGP框架，在去噪链的每个中间步骤学习前缀值函数（prefix value function），通过类Bellman风格的递归关系进行更新。该前缀值函数有两个用途：（1）作为辅助训练目标，促使中间去噪输出也成为高质量动作；（2）作为测试时的早停规则，当额外去噪步骤不太可能带来有意义改进时终止去噪过程。

Result: 在四个MuJoCo环境中与12个基线对比，POGP将所需去噪迭代次数减少约2.7倍，同时保持接近完整的任务性能。与最先进的动态扩散基线相比，前缀训练还将最终任务性能提升约3.5%。

Conclusion: 对中间去噪步骤进行监督不仅有利于自适应早停，还可以作为辅助目标来提升学习到的策略质量，为扩散策略的计算效率与性能提升提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Diffusion policies are a powerful policy class for continuous control, but their iterative denoising process creates a substantial computational bottleneck. Reducing this cost requires adapting the number of denoising steps to the difficulty of each action while preserving task performance. We introduce Prefix-Optimal Generative Policies (POGP), a framework that learns a prefix value function at every intermediate denoising step through a Bellman-style recursion over the denoising chain. The prefix value function serves two purposes: it provides an auxiliary training objective that encourages intermediate outputs to become high-quality actions, and it enables a test-time stopping rule that terminates denoising when additional steps are unlikely to produce meaningful improvement. Across four MuJoCo environments and comparisons with 12 baselines, POGP reduces the required number of denoising iterations by approximately 2.7-fold while retaining near-full task performance. Compared with state-of-the-art dynamic diffusion baselines, prefix training also improves final task performance by approximately 3.5%. These results indicate that supervising intermediate denoising steps is useful not only for adaptive early stopping, but also as an auxiliary objective that improves the learned policy.

</details>


### [114] [MALT: Lightweight Curvature-Aware Muon via Diagonal Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05088)
*Tongle Wu,Huanyu Dong,Ying Sun,Ziye Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MALT（Muon增强的轻量级双侧预条件）及其变体MALTER，通过轻量级对角预条件器显式捕捉损失曲面的曲率几何，减少Muon优化器对曲率各向异性的敏感性，在GPT-2大规模预训练中超越Muon且内存和计算开销几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon通过牛顿-舒尔茨迭代对动量矩阵进行正交化来缓解梯度各向异性，但它没有显式考虑损失曲面的曲率几何，仍可能对曲率各向异性敏感。本文希望弥补这一缺陷，在不显著增加开销的前提下提升优化器的鲁棒性和收敛性能。

Method: MALT使用双侧对角预条件器以低内存和计算开销近似捕捉损失曲面的曲率几何，对预条件后的动量进行牛顿-舒尔茨正交化，再映射回定义更新方向，并通过范数嫁接（norm grafting）控制更新幅度。MALTER进一步引入自适应步长重缩放（Adaptive stEpsize Rescaling）以增强对随机梯度噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small、Medium和Large预训练实验中，MALT和MALTER均优于Muon，同时保持了几乎相同的内存占用和墙钟时间。收敛性保证在随机非凸设定下已提供。

Conclusion: MALT系列通过轻量级曲率感知的双侧预条件有效弥补了Muon对曲率各向异性不敏感的短板，在保持高效性的同时显著提升了大语言模型预训练的性能，为该类优化器提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a promising alternative to AdamW for language model pretraining by orthogonalizing momentum matrices using Newton-Schulz iterations. Although Muon mitigates gradient anisotropy, it does not explicitly account for the curvature geometry of the loss landscape and may therefore remain sensitive to curvature anisotropy. We bridge this gap by proposing MALT (Muon Augmented by Lightweight Two-sided Preconditioning), which uses lightweight diagonal preconditioners to reduce the sensitivity of Muon to curvature anisotropy. Specifically, MALT uses two-sided diagonal preconditioners with low memory and computational overhead to approximately capture the curvature geometry of the loss landscape. It orthogonalizes the preconditioned momentum using Newton-Schulz iterations and maps the result back to define the update direction, while norm grafting controls the update magnitude. To improve the robustness of MALT to stochastic gradient noise, we further propose MALTER (MALT with Adaptive stEpsize Rescaling). Convergence guarantees are provided for MALT in the stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on GPT-2 Small, Medium, and Large pretraining show that the proposed methods outperform Muon while maintaining nearly the same memory footprint and wall-clock time.

</details>


### [115] [Multimodal Spatiotemporal Atmospheric Data Assimilation with Latent Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05103)
*Dibyajyoti Chakraborty,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜在视频流匹配（latent video flow-matching）的新型大气数据同化方法，利用ERA5再分析数据训练先验模型生成8天时间窗口内的一致轨迹，并通过后验采样吸收真实观测数据，可灵活完成滤波、平滑和集合预报等多种任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化方法依赖于数值预报模型和贝叶斯推断，计算成本高且框架复杂。作者希望提出一种根本不同的、统一的DA方法，能够直接从稀疏观测生成全状态集合预报，并同时支持滤波和平滑等多种同化任务。

Method: 使用潜在视频流匹配模型，在ERA5再分析数据（69个变量，8天窗口）上训练先验，生成时间上一致的轨迹；采用后验采样同化来自NOAA IGRA和ISD的真实观测数据；通过改变观测帧来灵活实现滤波和平滑任务。

Result: 该方法能够直接从稀疏观测生成全状态集合预报，性能与最先进的观测到预报（observation-to-forecast）模型相当。通过观测与未观测帧之间的信息传播，实现了多种DA任务的统一。

Conclusion: 该研究证明基于生成式视频模型的DA框架是可行的，提供了一种统一、灵活且具有竞争力的数据同化替代方案，能够同时支持滤波、平滑和集合预报。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) uses Bayesian inference to update the state of a numerical forecast model with observed data. In this study, we propose a fundamentally different, unified approach to atmospheric data assimilation. We use latent video flow-matching to sample temporally consistent trajectories from a prior trained using ERA5 reanalysis (69 variables over an 8-day window). We also use posterior sampling to assimilate real observation sources, such as those from the NOAA Integrated Global Radiosonde Archive and the Integrated Surface Database. Because the prior generates a continuous trajectory, it naturally propagates information between observed and unobserved frames. Therefore, we can perform various DA tasks, such as filtering and smoothing, simply by changing the observed frames. Moreover, we generate full-state ensemble forecasts directly from sparse observations, achieving performance competitive with state-of-the-art observation-to-forecast models.

</details>


### [116] [BnBERT-iPET: Sparse Few-Shot Language Modeling for Bengali via Lottery Ticket Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05104)
*Sajib Hossain,Md Kamrus Samad,Anan Ghosh,Labib Imam Chowdhury,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BnBERT-iPET，一种用于孟加拉语的稀疏Few-shot语言建模方法，通过迭代模式利用训练和彩票假设剪枝技术，仅保留原始BERT模型10%的边缘（90%稀疏度），在下游任务上可与Bangla Electra、Indic-BERT和XLM-RoBERTa等大型模型相媲美，大幅降低了计算成本和碳足迹，适用于资源受限语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练模型（如BERT）虽然性能卓越，但训练昂贵、耗时、碳足迹大，难以在低计算能力设备上运行，这对孟加拉语等资源受限语言构成了训练复杂模型的障碍。因此需要一种轻量化的few-shot学习方法，在保持性能的同时大幅减少模型规模。

Method: 提出BnBERT-iPET框架，结合两种核心技术：(1) 迭代模式利用训练（iPET，iterative Pattern Exploiting Training）实现few-shot学习；(2) 彩票假设（Lottery Ticket Hypothesis）剪枝实现90%稀疏度，仅保留初始BERT模型10%的边缘，最终得到轻量级稀疏few-shot学习语言模型。

Result: 实验表明，仅保留10%边缘的BnBERT-iPET模型在孟加拉语标准基准数据集的下游任务上，性能与更大的模型（如Bangla Electra、Indic-BERT、XLM-RoBERTa）不相上下，同时实现了90%的稀疏度，显著降低了存储和推理开销。

Conclusion: 稀疏剪枝结合few-shot学习能够在资源受限语言上实现与大模型相当的性能，证明了"并非所有边缘都同等重要"的假设在孟加拉语NLP任务中成立，为低资源语言的模型轻量化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have shown impressive success in NLP tasks owing to their complex structure and huge number of edges. Achieving state-of-the-art performance in natural language processing with a large pre-trained model such as BERT is expensive and time-consuming, carries a large carbon footprint, and is difficult to realize on machines with minimal computational capability. This creates a barrier to training complex models for resource-constrained languages such as Bengali. However, in a complex neural model, not all edges are equally impactful, and the contributions of some of them can be neglected. Pruning promises to reduce the memory footprint of regular networks, shorten the training time of ever-growing networks, and increase inference efficiency without sacrificing comparable performance. In this work, we introduce BnBERT-iPET, a sparse few-shot language modeling approach for Bengali, and experimentally show that a lightweight few-shot-learned language model retaining only 10% of the edges of an initial model such as BERT can perform neck and neck with much larger models on challenging tasks for a resource-constrained language such as Bengali. By learning from few shots through iterative pattern exploiting training and achieving 90% sparsity with the Lottery Ticket Hypothesis pruning technique, our pruned BnBERT-iPET model proves to be a tough competitor to state-of-the-art language models such as Bangla Electra, Indic-BERT, and XLM-RoBERTa on downstream tasks over standard benchmark datasets of the Bengali language.

</details>


### [117] [Reward Structure Shapes the Interaction Between Episodic Exploration and Neural Memory in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05111)
*Jai Malegaonkar,Rohan Patil,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统研究了部分可观测强化学习中探索奖励与记忆架构的交互作用，发现相同的探索奖励在不同记忆获取方式下产生三种截然不同的交互模式，并提出了基于观测锚定奖励机的新形式化框架来解释这些差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测强化学习中的智能体面临双重瓶颈：需要探索以发现奖励状态，同时需要记忆以保留经验并优化策略。然而，探索奖励和记忆架构传统上被孤立评估，二者的交互从未被测量。此外，标准的稀疏奖励概念混淆了时间信号密度与奖励实际监督的内容，缺乏对奖励结构本质的清晰刻画。

Method: 作者设计了受控实验，在三个环境中交叉组合情节式探索奖励与多种神经记忆架构（如LSTM、注意力等），这三个环境在记忆内容的获取方式上有所变化（需自行发现并保留、单一奖励监督线索、纯粹计划性观测）。同时通过受控奖励操作（如引入可避免的小惩罚）来验证奖励结构的作用，并提出观测锚定奖励机（Observation-Anchored Reward Machines）的形式化框架，区分结构稀疏性和潜在稀疏性。

Result: 研究识别出三种交互模式：当记忆内容需要主动发现和无监督保留时，探索奖励放大架构间能力差异；当记忆内容一旦被寻找到即为单一奖励监督线索时，探索奖励将所有架构拉平到一个共同上限；当观测流纯粹是计划性时，探索奖励完全无效。受控奖励操作验证了这些模式追踪的是奖励结构而非密度：密集奖励只有直接监督所需潜在记忆时才能中和探索奖励的效果；可避免的小惩罚会诱导策略收敛到次优驻态，而任一探索奖励都能解决该问题。

Conclusion: 探索和记忆是互补而非替代关系：探索奖励促使智能体产生暴露，但只有记忆能力才能将暴露转化为回报。新提出的稀疏性形式化词汇按记忆保留负担将三种实验机制进行了组织，为设计更有效的部分可观测强化学习算法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In partially observable reinforcement learning, agents face a dual bottleneck: they must explore to encounter rewarding states and retain that experience in memory to optimize their policies. Exploration bonuses and memory architectures are traditionally evaluated in isolation, leaving their interaction unmeasured, and standard notions of sparse reward conflate temporal signal density with what the reward actually supervises. We present a controlled study crossing episodic exploration bonuses with diverse neural memory architectures across three environments that vary how the content of memory is acquired. An identical bonus signal yields three distinct interaction patterns: it amplifies architectural capacity differences where memory content must be actively discovered and retained unsupervised; equalizes architectures to a shared ceiling where the content, once sought out, is a single reward-supervised cue; and is null where the observation stream is purely scheduled. Controlled reward manipulations verify that these patterns track reward structure rather than density: a dense reward neutralizes a bonus only if it directly supervises the required latent memory, and a small avoidable penalty on exploratory actions (leaving the optimum unchanged) induces policy convergence to suboptimal stationary states, which either bonus resolves. We then formalize reward sparsity with observation-anchored reward machines, separating structural sparsity (an automaton reproduces the return without the task-required history) from potential sparsity (the one-step reward misprices local exploratory actions); the resulting vocabulary organizes the three regimes by the retention burden each task exposes. Together, these results show exploration and memory are complements, not substitutes: a bonus induces exposure, and only memory converts exposure into return.

</details>


### [118] [DASyR-LLM: Domain-Aware Symbolic Regression with LLMs for Kinetic Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05120)
*Roberto Aliaga Medina,Paulina Quintanilla,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM引导的符号回归框架，将大语言模型嵌入迭代符号回归算法中，用于化学工程领域的动力学模型自动发现。LLM在每个迭代中扮演两个角色：对最优SR候选模型进行定性物理化学批判，以及基于SR生成模型和化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。在四个复杂度递增的in silico案例（涵盖多相催化和生物过程）中，该框架相比最先进的SR框架将识别真实模型的迭代次数减少41.7%-79.3%，且LLM在超过半数的引导运行中直接提出正确模型结构，同时在独立验证集上保持等效的预测性能（R²>0.98）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学模型发现是化学工程中的核心挑战，符号回归(SR)虽然强大但通常缺乏领域知识，经常探索物化上不合理的模型。大语言模型(LLM)为向搜索中注入领域专业知识提供了有前景的途径。作者希望通过LLM引导SR，减少探索不合理模型的迭代次数，从而降低湿实验成本。

Method: 作者提出了LLM-guided SR框架，将LLM模块嵌入迭代SR算法中。在每个迭代中LLM执行两个任务：(1)对最优SR候选模型进行定性物理化学批判；(2)受SR生成模型和嵌入化学知识启发，提出新的候选速率表达式。框架在四个复杂度递增的in silico案例上进行评估，涵盖多相催化和生物过程系统，并进行消融研究分析SR组件和LLM规模的影响。

Result: LLM引导框架将识别真实模型的迭代次数相比最先进SR框架减少41.7%-79.3%，LLM在超过半数引导运行中直接提出正确模型结构。两个方法在独立验证集上的预测性能等效，所有案例R²>0.98。消融研究表明SR组件和LLM规模都对性能有贡献，缩小版LLM仍能保持大部分发现效率。

Conclusion: LLM能有效将领域知识注入科学模型发现过程，为全自动、领域感知的动力学建模流程铺平道路，在需要湿实验迭代的实际场景中可显著减少实验工作量。

Abstract: Kinetic model discovery is a central challenge in chemical engineering, as accurate rate expressions are essential for understanding and controlling chemical and biological processes. Symbolic regression (SR) has emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for identifying interpretable kinetic models, but usually operates without domain knowledge, often exploring physicochemically implausible models. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for injecting domain expertise into this search. Here, we introduce an LLM-guided SR framework, embedding an LLM module within an iterative SR algorithm for automated kinetic model discovery. The LLM performs two roles at each iteration: (1) a qualitative physicochemical critique of the best SR candidates, and (2) the proposal of new candidate rate expressions guided by the SR-generated models and embedded chemical knowledge. Our framework is evaluated on four in silico case studies of increasing complexity, spanning heterogeneous catalysis and bioprocess systems. Results show the LLM-guided framework reduces iterations to identify the ground-truth model by $41.7-79.3\%$ versus a state-of-the-art SR framework, with the LLM directly proposing the correct model structure in over half of the guided runs. In practical settings, where each iteration typically requires a new wet-lab experiment, this translates into a substantial reduction in experimental effort. Predictive performance on an independent validation set is equivalent between both approaches, with $R^2>0.98$ in all case studies. Ablation studies indicate that both the SR component and the LLM scale contribute to this performance, with a reduced-size LLM largely retaining discovery efficiency. These findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively inject domain knowledge into scientific model discovery, paving the way toward fully automated, domain-aware kinetic modelling pipelines.

</details>


### [119] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Adam与梯度下降在低秩隐式偏差上的本质差异源于规范对称性（gauge symmetry）下的等变性。梯度下降等通过共同标量预条件（common-scalar preconditioning）的优化器具备规范等变性，从而继承低秩恢复机制；而Adam、RMSProp等逐坐标优化器不满足等变性，破坏低秩偏差。通过结构定理、传递定理以及九个更新规则的实证验证，系统地确立了各向异性（anisotropy）是低秩偏差丧失的根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同优化器在低秩矩阵恢复/感应任务中隐式偏差的差异根源，尤其是Adam与梯度下降从相同小初始化出发却产生截然不同的低秩行为的原因。

Method: 从损失函数在(U,V)→(UQ,VQ)规范变换下的不变性出发，建立规范等变性（gauge equivariance）作为优化器继承梯度流低秩机制的必要条件。提出结构定理（memoryless等变规则恰好对应Gram-决定的左预条件器）和传递定理（将梯度流的路径性质推广到共同标量流）。在欠定矩阵感应上排序九个更新规则的恢复误差，并引入从逐坐标到共享标量的单参数族及"谱调度"（spectral schedule）概念，最后在transformer和超光谱数据集上进行验证。

Result: 梯度下降、动量、共享标量Adam、Muon、Shampoo满足规范等变性；Adam、RMSProp及其他逐坐标方法不满足。单参数族从逐坐标到共享标量预条件单调地恢复低秩偏差，证明各向异性是原因。谱调度统一了关于Muon的矛盾报告：等速率更新恢复精确低秩目标但在谱尾增长时失效。在transformer中，Adam在第一步就分离两个规范等价初始化，而等变优化器停留在浮点精度；最终每头不变量W_Q^TW_K相对Frobenius距离相差56%。在超光谱数据集上，梯度下降在匹配训练损失下将留出误差降低43-44%。

Conclusion: 优化器的基选择（basis choice）不是调参细节，而是决定其选择哪个插值（interpolant）的关键决策。规范等变性是优化器继承梯度流低秩恢复机制的必要条件，而Adam等逐坐标方法因各向异性预条件而丧失该偏差。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [A Long-Run Persistence Theory for AI Systems under the Redundancy-Adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04012)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI系统长期持久性分析框架，基于冗余调整的人工年龄得分（AAS），将静态评估指标推广为循环级泛函，证明AI系统可以在无限循环运行中保持结构年龄有界，无需承受无界结构老化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统日益需要在重复交互、适应与更新的循环中运行，而非孤立的一次性输出。这引出一个根本性理论问题：AI系统能否无限期持续而不产生无界的结构老化？现有静态评估工具（如AAS）无法回答该问题，需要从静态度量发展为跨循环的理论框架。

Method: 本文将AAS从静态评估度量扩展为循环级泛函，生成跨多次运行的年龄序列。在每个循环中，结构年龄通过考虑冗余的加权对数惩罚函数定义于组件一致性水平之上。在此基础上建立了一系列渐近体制的层级定义（负担持久性、零负担持久性、振荡持久性、累积终末负担），并证明了比较排序、敏感性界、分量稳定化下的收敛性、有限全变差下的持久性、阻尼循环间扰动下的几何稳定化、以及非退化冗余条件下的零负担刻画等理论结果。

Result: 核心结果表明，无限循环延续并不需要无界的结构老化：AI系统可以经历无限多个循环而结构年龄保持有界；在更强正则条件下，边际老化消失；在最强体制下，循环级负担收敛至零。此外建立了各渐近体制之间的层级顺序及多种收敛与稳定性定理。

Conclusion: 该框架为将长期AI持久性视为有界结构负担问题而非不可避免的累积恶化问题提供了形式化基础，改变了AI系统长期运行的评估视角。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly expected to operate over repeated cycles of interaction, adaptation, and update rather than through isolated one-shot outputs. This raises a fundamental theoretical question: can an AI system persist indefinitely without incurring unbounded structural aging? This paper develops a long-run persistence framework for AI systems based on the redundancy-adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS). The model extends AAS from a static evaluative measure into a cycle-level functional that generates an age sequence across repeated operation. At each cycle, structural age is defined through a weighted, redundancy-aware logarithmic penalty over component consistency levels. Within this framework, cycle-level age is shown to be well defined and uniformly bounded, thereby excluding explosive pointwise aging. On this basis, the paper defines a hierarchy of asymptotic regimes, including burdened persistence, zero-burden persistence, oscillatory persistence, and cumulative terminal burden. It also establishes comparative ordering, sensitivity bounds, convergence under componentwise stabilization, persistence under finite total variation, geometric stabilization under damped inter-cycle perturbations, and a zero-burden characterization under nondegenerate redundancy conditions. The main result is that indefinite cyclic continuation does not require unbounded structural aging: an AI system may pass through infinitely many cycles while its structural age remains bounded, while under stronger regularity conditions its marginal aging vanishes and, in the strongest regime, its cycle-level burden converges to zero. The framework thus provides a formal basis for analyzing long-run artificial persistence as a problem of bounded structural burden rather than inevitable cumulative deterioration.

</details>


### [121] [The LLM Proposes, the Executive Disposes: A Self-Verifying Agent Instrument that Dissociates Commitment Drift from Binding Drift in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04066)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化的智能体验证仪器，通过确定性执行器掌控信念、语言模型只能提交类型化提案、预测在行动前预注册并由代码对观察进行匹配来确保验证的结构性而非事后性。仪表具备自失效机制和渲染不可见的影子参考，实现了干净的单变量消融实验，并报告了承诺机制消融导致目标放弃率从0.00升至1.00而绑定错误保持0.00的结果，同时透明披露任务有效性为零的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体的自身状态和自报告不可信，因此需要一种结构性而非事后性的验证方法，使得验证本身被嵌入智能体架构之中。

Method: 确定性执行器（Executive）拥有全部信念；语言模型只能提交类型化提案；主张仅在行动前预注册的预测与代码对观察的匹配之后才被接纳。体系包含自失效机制（写错误、渲染大小、盐渍金丝雀回显下限被破坏即自无效）和渲染不可见的影子参考（编译完整系统在每个消融单元中会承诺的计划，从而在机制被移除的单元也定义漂移度量）。

Result: 前八个架构运行中有四个被判定无效，每个都定位到真实缺陷；消融承诺机制后目标放弃率从0.00翻转为1.00，而绑定错误保持0.00（每单元三种子、每运行最多394个参考节拍、每次运行门控有效）；绑定通道修复被消融后未出现每节拍漂移，因为绑定是代码所有的，失败类别被结构性吸收，残余仅上游表现为假设形成的坍缩。全部52次门控运行在ARC-AGI-3上零级完成，任务效能为零。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是面向智能体开发的验证方法论及其可量化的漂移分解，尽管任务效能为零（作为预注册的结构性失败者），验证方法本身的有效性被清晰展示。

Abstract: How do you verify a long-horizon agent when its own state and self-reports are exactly what you cannot trust? We present an agent instrument built so that verification is structural rather than post-hoc. A deterministic Executive owns all belief; a language model may only file typed proposals, and a claim is admitted only when a prediction pre-registered before acting is matched against observation by code. Two properties make the instrument a verifier of its own science, not just of the agent: every run invalidates itself when per-organ write-error, render-size, or salted-canary-echo floors are breached (four of the first eight architecture runs were invalidated, each localizing a real defect); and a render-invisible shadow reference compiles the plan the full system would have committed in every ablation cell, so drift metrics are defined even where the mechanism under test has been removed. Using this instrument we report a clean, single-variable result on a failure every long-horizon agent suffers: ablating the commitment mechanism flips goal-abandonment from 0.00 to 1.00 while binding error stays flat at 0.00 (three seeds per cell, up to 394 reference beats per run, every run gated valid). The binding channel, by contrast, does not reappear as per-beat drift when its repair is ablated -- because binding is code-owned, the failure class is structurally absorbed, its only residue appearing one layer upstream as a collapse in hypothesis formation. We report these under full disclosure that task efficacy is null (zero level completions across 52 gated runs on ARC-AGI-3), pre-registered as a structural defeater. The contribution is a verification methodology for agent development and the drift decomposition it makes measurable.

</details>


### [122] [Monte Carlo Tree Search for Table-to-Multimodal Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04071)
*Teng Lin,Zhiyang Zhang,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MCTS-Report，一个基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）驱动的框架，将多模态表格到报告生成视为在结构化搜索空间上的渐进构建过程。通过将报告生成分解为章节规划、可视化任务识别、图表生成、洞察组织和叙述精炼等原子动作，并配合多维奖励函数（数值事实一致性、图表质量、图表-文本对齐、结构完整性）进行搜索引导，同时在MMRBench基准上显著超越强基线，整体得分77.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从结构化表格数据自动生成包含文本分析和可视化图表的专业多模态报告是数据智能中的关键挑战。现有方法受限于固定的线性流程和孤立的子任务处理，无法实现对事实准确性、视觉质量和叙述连贯性的联合优化。

Method: 提出MCTS-Report框架：1) 将报告生成分解为原子动作（章节规划、可视化任务识别、图表生成、洞察组织、叙述精炼），每个动作由LLM基于当前报告状态的动态推理执行；2) 在MCTS过程中让LLM生成逐步推理和动作，将推理轨迹存储在节点中以实现上下文感知的连贯报告构建；3) 设计多维奖励函数，联合评估SQL验证的数值事实一致性、图表质量、图表-文本对齐和结构完整性，并加入多样性惩罚抑制重复图表、前置条件检查剪枝无效动作；4) 构建包含六个领域真实表格的MMRBench基准。

Result: 在MMRBench基准上的实验表明，MCTS-Report在结构完整性、数值准确性、图表-文本对齐和洞察新颖性方面显著优于强基线，整体得分达到77.9。

Conclusion: MCTS-Report通过将多模态报告生成建模为结构化搜索空间上的渐进构建过程，有效克服了传统方法线性流水线无法联合优化各子任务的局限，实现了更高的事实准确性和报告质量。

Abstract: Automatically generating professional multimodal reports comprising both textual analysis and visual charts from structured tabular data is a critical challenge in data intelligence. Existing methods suffer from fixed linear pipelines and isolated subtask processing, which hinder joint optimization of factual accuracy, visual quality, and narrative coherence. To address these issues, this paper proposes MCTS-Report, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-driven framework that formulates multimodal table-to-report generation as a progressive construction process over a structured search space. The core idea is to decompose report generation into atomic actions, including chapter planning, visualization task identification, chart generation, insight organization, and narrative refinement, each executed by an LLM based on dynamic reasoning conditioned on the current report state. We use an LLM to generate step-by-step reasoning and actions during MCTS, storing the reasoning trajectory in each node for context-aware, coherent report construction. To guide the search, we design a multi-dimensional reward function that jointly evaluates numerical fact consistency (via SQL), chart quality, chart-text alignment, and structural completeness, while incorporating a diversity penalty to suppress repeated charts and a precondition check to prune invalid actions. We also construct MMRBench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising real-world tables from six domains, paired with expert-refined reference report structures and verifiable key insights. Experiments on MMRBench demonstrate that MCTS-Report significantly outperforms strong baselines across structural completeness, numerical accuracy, chart-text alignment, and insight novelty, achieving a 77.9 overall score.

</details>


### [123] [FinProBench: Evaluating Financial AI Agents with Role-Grounded Rubrics Derived from Professional Deliverables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04077)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinProBench基准和Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC)管道，用于金融AI代理的评估。RGRC从从业者的交付物中提取隐性标准来构建评分细则，覆盖57个职业、1,723个交付物。实验显示，在角色专业化任务中RGRC（99.1%）远超Prompt-only（78.0%），而在传统角色中两者接近（90.7% vs 89.2%），表明专业从业者标准对超越模型先验的评估至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融AI代理评估的rubric方法通常从任务提示或模型输出中推导标准，忽略了从业者交付物中体现的隐性专业标准（tacit standards），这些标准难以从提示或输出中直接获取。需要一种与真实专业工作对齐的评估标准。

Method: 提出RGRC四阶段管道：交付物收集（Deliverable Collection）、能力提取（Competency Extraction）、细则综合（Rubric Synthesis）和验证（Validation）。将57个职业按交付物类型分为30个先验丰富的传统角色和27个先验稀疏的专业角色。构建FinProBench基准，包含1,723个精选交付物、8个金融子行业、161种交付物类型，并用异构LLM评委和角色级细则进行评估。

Result: 在传统角色中Prompt-only与RGRC性能接近（89.2% vs 90.7%），但在角色专业化角色中RGRC显著领先（99.1% vs 78.0%）。人类交付物平均得分最高（73.7分），四个AI系统得分在69.6-70.3之间且置信区间重叠。角色级细则复用使每任务构建成本降低6.7倍。

Conclusion: 提示工程可以在模型先验充分覆盖传统规范时近似细则效果，但超出先验的角色专业化标准需要来自专业从业者的落地依据（grounding）。角色级rubric复用大幅降低构建成本，为金融AI代理评估提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Evaluating financial AI agents requires criteria aligned with real professional work. Existing rubric methods typically derive criteria from task prompts or model outputs, overlooking tacit standards visible only in practitioner deliverables. We introduce FinProBench, a benchmark for professional financial tasks, and Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC), a reusable pipeline that derives rubrics from deliverables produced by practitioners in the same role. RGRC comprises four stages: Deliverable Collection, Competency Extraction, Rubric Synthesis, and Validation. Its rubrics capture tacit standards, distinguish quality levels, and transfer across tasks within a role. Before analysis, we classified 57 occupations by deliverable genre into 30 prior-rich conventional roles and 27 prior-sparse role-specialized roles. Across all roles, Prompt-only nearly matches RGRC for conventional roles (89.2% vs. 90.7%), but RGRC substantially outperforms it for role-specialized roles (99.1% vs. 78.0%). This split indicates that prompt engineering can approximate rubrics when conventions are well represented in model priors, while professional grounding is essential for standards beyond those priors. FinProBench is built from 1,723 curated deliverables spanning 57 occupations, 8 financial sub-industries, and 161 deliverable types, and releases an initial evaluation set of 20 complete tasks covering 20 roles in 7 sub-industries. With heterogeneous LLM judges and role-level rubrics, human deliverables rank first on average (73.7 vs. 70.3, 70.2, and 69.6 out of 100), while all four systems show overlapping 95% confidence intervals and complementary strengths. Reusing rubrics at the role level reduces estimated per-task construction effort by 6.7 times relative to authoring each rubric from scratch.

</details>


### [124] [FinPerMA: A Theory-Informed, Event-Grounded Personalized-Memory Benchmark for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04095)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinPerMA，一个基于事件驱动的个性化记忆基准测试框架，用于评估LLM智能体在长期纵向金融轨迹中维护和更新个性化用户模型的能力。实验显示，七个前沿LLM和多种记忆配置在2994个问题上均远未饱和，摘要式记忆虽保留事实但丢失个性化偏好信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化记忆基准测试主要关注事实性记忆保留或依赖约束较弱的模型生成轨迹，未能充分探索事件驱动的偏好适应性。在金融咨询等高风险领域，LLM智能体作为个性化助手需要长期维护和更新个体化用户模型，但这一能力尚不明确。

Method: 构建事件驱动的FinPerMA基准：生成流程结合确定性、理论驱动的冲击影响规则，受控LLM叙述和自动化质量筛选；设置Post-Shock检查点以隔离智能体是否已将重大事件整合进持久用户模型。数据集包含276个用户画像和2994个问题，评估包含七个前沿LLM和最多七种记忆配置。

Result: 所有全上下文配置在整体准确率上不超过约0.47，多项选择题准确率不超过约39%，远未饱和。归因分析显示摘要式记忆常保留事实细节却丢失个性化所需的偏好信号，简单检索方法可胜过专用记忆系统，且冲击后差距扩大。

Conclusion: 现有LLM智能体在长期个性化记忆维护和事件驱动偏好更新方面存在显著不足，当前"事实保留型"记忆方案与真正个性化需求之间存在鸿沟，需要更有效的记忆设计来捕获和更新用户的偏好信号。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used as personalized assistants in high-stakes domains such as financial advising, yet it remains unclear whether they can maintain and update an individualized user model over long horizons. Existing personalized-memory benchmarks primarily test factual retention or rely on weakly constrained model-generated trajectories, leaving event-driven preference adaptation underexplored. We introduce FinPerMA, an event-grounded benchmark that evaluates personalized memory against frozen longitudinal investor trajectories. Its generation pipeline combines deterministic, theory-informed impact rules, controlled LLM narration, and automated quality screening; a Post-Shock checkpoint isolates whether an agent has integrated a material event into its persistent user model. On 2,994 questions from 276 personas, seven frontier LLMs and up to seven memory configurations remain far from saturated: no full-context configuration exceeds approximately 0.47 overall accuracy or approximately 39% on multiple-choice questions. Attribution analysis shows that summary-based memory often preserves factual details while losing the preference signals needed for personalization; simple retrieval can therefore outperform purpose-built memory systems, with the gap widening after shocks.

</details>


### [125] [BrainBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Comprehensive EEG Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04156)
*Yangxuan Zhou,Sha Zhao,Yuning Chen,Chen Wu,Jiquan Wang,Shijian Li,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EEGBench，一个用于全面、指令条件化脑电（EEG）理解的大规模统一基准，包含四个子集、17个数据集、多个任务和大量真实数据实例，评估多种LLM在两种执行范式下的综合EEG能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG分析评估主要聚焦于孤立的解码任务或系统特定演示，缺乏对大语言模型在全面EEG理解（连接自然语言指令、信号处理、定量证据和科学解释）这一综合能力上的系统性量化。

Method: 构建统一基准EEGBench，包含Foundational Analysis、Sleep Assessment、Neurocognitive Assessment和Physiological Integration四个子集，覆盖17个数据集、多个任务和大量真实数据实例。系统需根据指令和EEG记录（可选其他生理信号）执行分析并生成科学报告。采用数值、分类、集合、序列、语义及伪影六类验证方式评估输出。在两种范式下评估多个代表性LLM：CodeAct自主代码执行和BrainAgent结构化智能体分析，总计超过10万次执行。

Result: 不同模型、子集、难度级别和执行范式之间的结果差异显著，表明EEG能力取决于模型本身及其操作化方式。基准代码和数据将公开发布，评估结果持续更新。

Conclusion: EEGBench为推进基于LLM的EEG理解提供了一个可复现的测试平台，填补了全面EEG理解能力评估的空白，揭示了模型选择和执行范式对EEG分析效果的关键影响。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis extends beyond assigning predefined labels to recordings; it requires workflows connecting natural-language instructions, signal processing, quantitative evidence, and scientific interpretation. We term this capability \emph{comprehensive EEG understanding}. Existing evaluations, however, primarily target isolated decoding tasks or system-specific demonstrations, leaving the competence of large language models (LLMs) insufficiently quantified. We introduce \benchmarkname{}, a unified benchmark for comprehensive, instruction-conditioned EEG understanding. It comprises four subsets---Foundational Analysis, Sleep Assessment, Neurocognitive Assessment, and Physiological Integration---covering 17 datasets, \numcases{} tasks, and over \numinstances{} real-data instances. Given an instruction and EEG recordings with optional physiological signals, a system must perform the analysis and produce a scientifically grounded report and, when required, artifacts. Outputs are assessed through numerical, categorical, set, sequence, semantic, and artifact validation. We evaluate \nummodels{} representative LLMs across more than 100K executions under two paradigms: autonomous code execution with CodeAct and structured agentic analysis with BrainAgent. Results vary substantially across models, subsets, difficulty levels, and execution paradigms, showing that EEG competence depends on the model and its operationalization. \benchmarkname{} provides a reproducible testbed for advancing LLM-based EEG understanding. The code and benchmark will be released soon, with evaluation results continuously updated.

</details>


### [126] [Adversarially Robust Abductive Fusion of Pre-trained Transformer-based Perception Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04190)
*Mario Leiva,Yue Ma,Qinru Qiu,Gerardo Simari,Paulo Shakarian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需领域知识的元认知层方法，利用向量空间几何构建基于标签向量池（LVP）的误差检测规则，通过整数规划将多个ViT检测器的预测视为一致性约束下的溯因问题求解，在天气偏移的航拍基准上达到与人工领域知识规则相当的性能，并对协调性标签翻转攻击具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练感知模型在新环境中因分布偏移而性能下降，简单的集成方法（如多数投票）无法恢复精度，存在召回率与精确率的权衡问题且对协调性故障脆弱。已有的元认知方法依赖手工编写的领域知识线索，无法迁移到真正新颖的场景，因此需要一种无需领域知识即可学习元认知层的方法。

Method: 1) 利用向量空间几何：为每个模型构建标签向量池（LVP），基于模型自身训练嵌入生成原型，从检测结果与训练原型之间的几何关系推导误差检测规则，无需任何领域知识；2) 保持神经符号特性：几何规则与领域知识规则共享同一逻辑框架，可互补融合；3) 将多个ViT检测器的融合建模为基于一致性的溯因问题，在测试时通过精确整数规划（IP）和多项式时间启发式求解。

Result: 在包含15个天气偏移测试集和6个ViT检测器的航拍基准上：领域知识无关层与最强多数投票变体在干净数据上F1差距在0.005以内；在90%标签翻转攻击率下平均F1为0.42，对比MV-Plurality的0.35（相对提升22%）；当翻转率超过0.4时，在每个测试集上均取得最高F1。

Conclusion: 基于向量空间几何的无领域知识元认知层可以达到与人工领域知识规则相当的误差检测性能，同时保持神经符号框架的灵活性（可补充领域知识），并在协调性攻击下显著优于多数投票基线，证明了该方法在分布偏移和对抗条件下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Deploying pre-trained perception models in novel environments degrades their accuracy under distributional shift, and assembling them alone does not recover it: combiners such as majority voting trade recall for precision and are brittle to coordinated failures. Prior metacognitive methods learn logical rules that flag a model's errors, but rely on hand-authored domain-knowledge cues (object-size priors, segmentation masks) that do not transfer to genuinely novel scenes. We show that this metacognitive layer can be learned without any domain knowledge by exploiting vector-space geometry: per-model Label Vector Pools (LVP), built from each model's own training embeddings, yield error-detection rules from the geometry of detections relative to training-determined prototypes, reaching parity with domain-knowledge rules to within $0.002$ every F1 on test set. Because the approach remains neurosymbolic, these geometric rules share a single logical framework and can still be complemented by domain knowledge when available. We frame the fusion of multiple imperfect ViT-based detectors as a consistency-based abduction problem solved at test time by an exact Integer Program (IP) and a polynomial-time heuristic. On an aerial-imagery benchmark of 15 weather-shifted test sets and six ViT detectors, our domain-knowledge-free layer matches the strongest majority-vote variant on clean data (within $0.005$ F1) and, unlike every majority-vote baseline, retains its performance under a coordinated label-flipping attack: at a $90\%$ flip rate it averages $0.42$ F1 versus $0.35$ for MV-Plurality (a $22\%$ relative gain) and attains the highest F1 on \emph{every} test set once the flip rate exceeds $0.4$

</details>


### [127] [MatrAIx: Simulating the World with 8.3 Billion Persona Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04205)
*Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Jianheng Hou,Jintao Huang,Qianfeng Wen,Shirley Huang,Yifan Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yilan Fan,Yijun Wang,Koutian Wu,Ruoqi Gao,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Jing Tang,Brihi Joshi,Heming Liu,Zheyuan Deng,Zonglin Di,Sankalp Jajee,Jiuyao Lu,Zhiwei Zhang,Saksham Kapoor,Ishan Gupta,Yunhan Zhao,Chanwoo Park,Yucheng Lu,Bing Hu,Weihang Xiao,Aravind Mohan,Hanwen Xing,Runyu Zhang,Mihir Kulshreshtha,Yuanda Xu,Qianyu Zhu,Dianzhuo Wang,Yuxin Xiao,Bowen Jiang,Yongye Su,Wenhao Chai,Zuxin Liu,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Ethan Ye,Shivam Patel,Jason Xie,Alex Martin Richmond,Weixiang Ding,Emre Okcular,Diya Mathew,Ziheng Wang,Rana M. Shahroz Khan,Zhejian Peng,Fang Wu,Fan Nie,Xinyang Han,Yubin Kim,Jiawei Zhang,Zhenting Qi,Huangyuan Su,Xu Pan,Abinitha Gourabathina,Hyewon Jeong,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Kumail Alhamoud,Kimia Hamidieh,Zidi Xiong,Samuel Schmidgall,Pengrui Han,Yepeng Huang,Yongheng Wang,Bowen Yang,Alex Gu,Yuchu Wang,Akshay Paruchuri,Brenna Li,Hejie Cui,Jiayuan Ding,Chaosheng Dong,Jiahao Wang,Yixuan He,Chi Wang,Pamela Bhattacharya,Tianyi Peng,Paul Pu Liang,Mitchell Gordon,Yilun Du,Marinka Zitnik,James Zou,Prasanna Tambe,Philip Torr,Emily Fox,Asu Ozdaglar,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MatrAIx是一个面向AI系统和数字产品的大规模模拟用户评测基础设施，包含83亿条个性化档案（Persona 8B）和约100万条高质量core set数据，提供Survey、AI Chatbot、Web和App四种评测环境以及覆盖25+领域的1,010个应用任务。通过18,189次评测试验验证，persona adherence率为91.5%，证明了以异构模拟用户进行端到端评测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对AI系统和数字产品的评测成本高昂、速度慢且难以规模化；离线评测虽更具可扩展性，但往往忽略了人类多样性和交互行为。作者希望构建一个能以异构用户大规模模拟评估AI系统的基础设施，弥补在线人工评估与离线评测之间的鸿沟。

Method: MatrAIx包含三大核心组件：1) Persona 8B——83亿条persona记录、1,290个分类维度，通过依赖图采样或从人类撰写的档案派生；释放约100万条质量过滤后的core set（599,847条人类接地+400,000条合成）；2) MatrAIx Playground——提供Survey、AI Chatbot、Web和App四种评测环境；3) 1,010个应用任务，覆盖Commerce、Software、Finance、Healthcare等25+领域。进行了18,189次评测试验，使用三个LLM（Claude Opus 4.8、GPT 5.5、Claude Haiku 4.5）驱动persona agents；通过400次对照实验验证persona adherence，并通过人类和LLM裁判评估persona提取质量。

Result: 在400次受控研究中，persona agents在十个行为属性、四种环境下有366次（91.5%）正确表达或正确抑制了声明行为。模拟反馈能够捕捉不同persona背景下的决策和偏好差异，包括价格上涨后的犹豫、AI助手失败后的继续意愿以及延迟容忍度等行为模式。

Conclusion: MatrAIx提供了端到端的评估基础设施，能够以多样化模拟用户评测AI系统和数字产品，为验证AI系统在异构用户群体中的表现提供了可规模化、可复现的评测手段。

Abstract: Human evaluation of AI systems and digital products is costly, slow, and difficult to scale. Offline evaluations are more scalable but often abstract away human diversity and interactive behavior. We therefore introduce MatrAIx, a population-scale simulated-user evaluation infrastructure for testing AI systems and digital products with heterogeneous users. MatrAIx has three core components: First, Persona 8B contains 8.3 billion persona records represented by 1,290 categorical dimensions. Records are either sampled from a dependency graph that preserves correlated attributes or derived from human-authored profiles. We release a quality-filtered coreset of approximately 1 million personas, comprising 599,847 human-grounded and 400,000 synthetic records. Second, the MatrAIx Playground provides four environments in which diverse users evaluate and interact with digital products: Survey, AI Chatbot, Web, and App. Third, MatrAIx provides 1,010 application tasks spanning more than 25 domains, including Commerce, Software, Finance, and Healthcare. We conducted 18,189 evaluation trials across eight representative tasks. Persona agents were powered by three LLMs: Claude Opus 4.8, GPT 5.5, and Claude Haiku 4.5. The resulting feedback captures how decisions and preferences vary across persona backgrounds, including hesitation after a price increase, willingness to continue after an AI assistant fails, and latency tolerance. We conducted two main validation studies: First, a 400-trial controlled study evaluated persona adherence across ten behavioral attributes and all four environments. The declared behavior was expressed or correctly suppressed in 366 trials (91.5%). Second, human and LLM judges evaluated the extraction quality of human-grounded personas. Overall, MatrAIx provides an end-to-end infrastructure for evaluating AI systems and digital products with diverse simulated human users.

</details>


### [128] [Interoceptive Attention as Dynamic Homeostatic Prioritization in a Foraging Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04232)
*St John Grimbly,Nicolas Kuske,Evert A. Boonstra,Bruce A. Bassett,Charel van Hoof,Rowan Hodson,Benjamin Rosman,Ryan Smith,Mark Solms,Jonathan P. Shock*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究生物体在有限感知带宽下如何分配感知精度以调节多种竞争性需求，提出一种基于主动推理的选择性内感受精度分配机制，并通过AffectWorld四通道觅食网格世界验证其能显著提升学习阶段存活率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统必须在有限感知带宽下管理相互竞争的需求，提升某一通道的估计精度会牺牲其他通道。任何固定预算的系统都必须决定如何分配感知精度，本文旨在探索这种选择性分配机制如何影响生物体的生存表现和学习动力学。

Method: 基于主动推理构建四通道内感受（多需求）觅食智能体。智能体每一步读取自身身体状态信念，识别最需要的通道，将固定预算的内感受精度重新分配至该通道，使同一精度形状的似然同时用于信念更新与规划。在AffectWorld（四通道觅食网格世界）进行仿真，与均匀精度分配智能体对比。

Result: 选择性精度分配使学习阶段存活率相比均匀精度智能体提升超过两倍（0.414 vs 0.199，跨11种布局，每布局32个种子，配对聚类bootstrap p≤10⁻⁴）。该收益部分通过规划而非仅感知实现——仅拒绝给规划器形状似然会消除约一半收益。收益与需求对齐——将精度指向最不需关注的通道表现差于均匀分配。被关注的通道学习自身动态速度快约两倍，即使在观测数匹配时仍保持领先，体现了精度路由的行为痕迹。

Conclusion: 选择性感知精度分配是固定预算感知系统下优化多需求生存的一种有效策略，其收益同时源于感知和规划通路，且精度路由在行为层面（学习速度而非存活率）留下可观测的追踪痕迹。

Abstract: Biological systems must regulate competing needs under limited perceptual bandwidth, where sharpening one estimate costs the capacity to sharpen the others. Any fixed-budget system therefore has to decide where to allocate its perceptual precision. We study this in a foraging agent that must keep several bodily needs satisfied to survive, modelled with active inference. At each step it reads its own body-state beliefs, identifies the most-needed channel, and reallocates a fixed budget of interoceptive precision toward it, so that the same precision-shaped likelihood feeds both belief update and planning. In AffectWorld, a four-channel foraging gridworld, this selective allocation more than doubles learning-phase survival at matched budget against a uniform-precision agent ($0.414$ vs $0.199$ across 11 layouts, $n{=}32$ seeds each, paired cluster-bootstrap $p \leq 10^{-4}$). Two further results sharpen the mechanism. The benefit runs through planning as well as perception, since denying the shaped likelihood to the planner alone removes about half of it. It is also need-aligned, since aiming precision at the least-needed channel does worse than spreading it evenly. The attended channel additionally learns its own dynamics about twice as fast, and stays ahead even at matched observation count, a behavioural trace of the same precision routing, visible in learning speed, not survival.

</details>


### [129] [The RAIL Principles for Neurosymbolic AI: Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04285)
*Agnese Chiatti,Michael Cochez,Cristina Cornelio,Sebastijan Dumancic,Artur d'Avila Garcez,Luis C. Lamb,Lia Morra,Mathias Niepert,Robert Peharz,Alberto Speranzon,Maarten Stol,Annette Ten Teije,Thiviyan Thanapalasingam,Frank Van Harmelen,Emile Van Krieken,Antonio Vergari,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出神经符号AI设计四大原则（RAIL：推理、保障、接口、学习），主张机器学习与形式推理的结合并非小众方法，而是构建可靠、高效、可信AI系统的关键。RAIL框架可统一分析多种AI系统（从物理感知机器学习到Alpha-*系列、因果学习及工具增强的大语言模型），为工程实践提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经符号AI日益受到关注，但其在设计生产级AI系统中的系统化方法论仍缺失。作者认为机器学习与符号推理的融合对高可靠性、低数据场景至关重要，需要一套统一的设计原则来指导AI系统架构决策。

Method: 提出RAIL四原则框架（Reasoning推理、Assurances保障、Interfacing接口、Learning学习），并以此为基础统一分析各类AI系统，包括物理感知机器学习、神经引导搜索（如Alpha-*）、因果学习以及工具增强的大语言模型等。

Result: 展示了RAIL框架能够为看似迥异的AI系统提供统一的视角，帮助工程师在设计和部署生产级AI系统时做出更明智、更有原则的决策。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法应成为下一代AI技术设计的核心范式，RAIL原则为将神经符号方法整合到实际AI系统中提供了有效的指导框架。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic AI systems that integrate machine learning and symbolic reasoning are rapidly gaining attention. They complement the data-intensive statistical approaches of neural networks and language models with symbolic reasoning algorithms to function in high-stakes domains or in low-data regimes that characterize many real-world applications. We argue that the neurosymbolic combination of machine learning and formal reasoning is not a niche approach within AI, but rather includes many already successful techniques that are of crucial importance to the development of reliable, efficient and, ultimately, trustworthy systems. This perspective prompts a re-examination of the design of current AI systems. We show that many leading AI systems, including some that are not traditionally considered as neurosymbolic, can be analysed from the perspective of four principles of neurosymbolic AI design: Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing and Learning (RAIL). Applying the RAIL framework offers a unified view of seemingly disparate AI systems, ranging from physics-aware machine learning to neuro-guided search (such as Google DeepMind's Alpha-* suite), causal learning and tool-augmented Large Language Models. Importantly, the RAIL principles will enable engineers to make better-informed and more principled decisions about the design and deployment of production-level AI systems. In this article, we introduce the RAIL principles, examine how they can be applied across major areas of AI, and illustrate how they may guide practitioners to integrate neurosymbolic methods into next-generation AI technologies.

</details>


### [130] [SafeCommit: Certifying When Memory-Grounded Agents May Safely Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04289)
*Mayur Akewar,Ravi Ranjan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeCommit是一个位于智能体推理与外部执行之间的风险控制层，通过构建校准的潜在世界集合并利用一致性动作证书，确保智能体只有在动作在所有保留世界中均安全时才执行，从而解决长时程智能体因记忆不确定而导致的过早承诺（premature commitment）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体依赖持久记忆和工具执行具有外部副作用的动作，核心失败模式是过早承诺——即在记忆接地（memory grounding）可能过时、冲突、不完整或损坏的情况下就采取行动。现有方法缺少对"何时证据充分足以安全行动"这一问题的显式形式化处理。

Method: SafeCommit提出：(1) 从记忆、观测、工具输出、来源信息（provenance）和策略约束中构建校准的潜在世界集合（plausible latent worlds）；(2) 仅当一致性动作证书（conformal action certificate）证明动作在所有保留世界中安全时才允许执行副作用动作；(3) 否则选择低副作用的探针（probe）动作来针对阻碍认证的世界进行探测，或返回保守的兜底方案；(4) 在校准的世界覆盖下，不安全认证提交的概率被界为α；在不完美世界提议下，误差界分解为校准误差和表征误差两部分。

Result: 提供了一个无依赖的受控模拟器，展示了安全性-效用权衡（safety-utility tradeoff），并可通过单条命令复现论文中所有报告结果。

Conclusion: 本文旨在提供一个具体方法，不仅回答智能体"应该做什么"（what to do），还回答"可用证据何时足以安全地去做"（when evidence is sufficient to safely do it），强调在外部执行前对记忆不确定性进行风险管理的重要性。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly use persistent memory and tools to take actions with external side effects. A central failure mode is premature commitment: an agent acts before resolving whether its memory grounding is stale, conflicting, incomplete, or corrupted. We formalize this problem as safe commitment under memory uncertainty and introduce SafeCommit, a risk controlled layer between agent reasoning and external execution. The layer constructs a calibrated set of plausible latent worlds from memory, observations, tool outputs, provenance, and policy constraints. It permits a side effectful action only when a conformal action certificate shows that the action is safe in every retained world. Otherwise, it selects a low-side-effect probe that targets the worlds blocking certification, or returns a conservative fallback. Under calibrated world coverage, the probability of an unsafe certified commit is at most the target level α; with imperfect world proposal, the bound separates calibration and representation error. A dependency-free controlled simulator illustrates the safety-utility tradeoff and reproduces all reported results with one command. The goal is to offer a concrete approach for deciding not only what an agent should do, but when the available evidence is sufficient to safely do it.

</details>


### [131] [NeuMoSync: End-to-End Neuromodulatory Control for Plasticity and Adaptability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04358)
*Seyed Roozbeh Razavi Rohani,Khashayar Khajavi,Wesley Chung,Mandana Samiei,Mo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuMoSync架构，通过在深度神经网络中引入神经元级动态调制和同步机制（灵感源自大脑全局神经调控），提升持续学习中的可塑性保持与知识迁移能力，在多种CL基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中深度神经网络常面临可塑性损失（plasticity loss）和知识迁移能力不足的问题，限制了模型的长期适应性。现有方法未能有效解决这一问题。

Method: NeuMoSync扩展标准神经网络架构，为每个神经元添加可学习的特征向量以追踪网络历史上下文，并引入一个在更高抽象层次运行的模块。该模块根据当前输入和网络动态状态合成神经元特定信号，自适应地调控激活动态和突触可塑性。

Result: 在记忆任务（Random Label CIFAR-10/MNIST）、概念漂移（Shuffle CIFAR-10/Mini-ImageNet）、类增量学习（Class Split ImageNet/CIFAR-100）和域增量学习（Permuted MNIST）等多个CL基准上，NeuMoSync在保持可塑性、前向/后向迁移方面均优于现有方法；消融实验验证了各组件必要性，学习到的调制信号展现出可解释的跨任务协调模式。

Conclusion: 将类脑全局协调机制融入深度学习系统能有效增强持续学习的鲁棒性和适应性，为设计更强大的自适应持续学习系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) requires models to learn tasks sequentially, yet deep neural networks often suffer from plasticity loss and poor knowledge transfer, which can impede their long-term adaptability. Drawing high-level inspiration from global neuromodulatory mechanisms in the brain, we introduce Neuromodulation and Synchronization (NeuMoSync), a novel architecture that integrates dynamic, neuron-specific modulation into deep neural networks to enhance their adaptability and plasticity. NeuMoSync extends standard neural network architectures with learnable feature vectors for each neuron that track network-wide historical context and with a module operating at a higher level of abstraction. This module synthesizes neuron-specific signals, conditioned on both current inputs and the network's evolving state, to adaptively regulate activation dynamics and synaptic plasticity. Evaluated on diverse CL benchmarks, including memorization (Random Label CIFAR-10 and Random Label MNIST), concept drift (Shuffle CIFAR-10 and Shuffle Mini-ImageNet), class-incremental learning (Class Split ImageNet and Class Split CIFAR-100), and domain-incremental learning (Permuted MNIST), NeuMoSync demonstrates strong performance in retaining plasticity and achieves improvements in both forward and backward adaptation compared with existing methods. Ablation studies validate the necessity of each component, while analysis of the learned modulatory signals reveals interpretable coordination patterns across tasks. Our work underscores the potential of integrating global coordination mechanisms into deep learning systems to advance robust, adaptive continual learning. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/RoozbehRazavi/NeuMoSync.

</details>


### [132] [Improving Auto-Design of Neural PDE Solvers with a Domain-Specific Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04384)
*Shengxin Kong,Liwen Xu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADSL-PDE提出了一种结构化搜索空间表示方法，将神经PDE求解器设计从"无限制Python代码生成"转变为"在紧凑的搜索状态空间中进化"，通过确定性编译器将搜索状态映射为可执行代码，使LLM驱动的自动设计聚焦于有效且关键的设计决策而非实现细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限制Python代码空间中，有效的神经PDE求解器极为稀疏——大多数候选程序存在语法错误、语义不兼容或数值不稳定问题。直接让LLM生成代码会浪费大部分搜索能力在解决实现失败上，而非推理求解器质量本身，这严重制约了搜索效率和优化效果。

Method: ADSL-PDE引入了一个介于求解器概念与可执行代码之间的结构化搜索状态。该状态涵盖决定神经PDE求解器性能的功能性决策维度：架构、物理约束、目标函数、采样和优化策略，同时抽象掉底层实现细节。一个确定性编译器将每个有效的搜索状态映射为可执行的求解器，从而重塑搜索空间——消除大量无效程序区域，增加有意义候选的密度，同时保留组合自由度以发现未见过的设计。基于此表示，进化代理通过经验反馈迭代地提出、评估和精炼搜索状态。

Result: 在多个PDE基准测试上，ADSL-PDE同时提升了搜索效率和优化稳定性，在前十次进化迭代内实现了超过52%的改进。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，LLM驱动的自动设计有效性的关键不仅在于更强的推理能力，更在于一种能将探索集中于有效且关键决策的搜索空间表示。ADSL-PDE的搜索空间重塑策略可能为更广泛的LLM自动设计问题提供通用原则。

Abstract: Neural PDE solver auto-design is fundamentally a search-space representation problem. In the space of unrestricted Python programs, valid solvers form an extremely sparse subset: most candidate programs are syntactically incorrect, semantically incompatible, or numerically unstable. Direct code generation therefore forces an LLM to spend most of its search capacity navigating implementation failures rather than reasoning about solver quality. ADSL-PDE addresses this challenge by introducing a structured search state between solver concepts and executable code. It represents the functional decisions that determine a neural PDE solver (architecture, physical constraints, objectives, sampling, and optimization) while abstracting away low-level implementation details. A deterministic compiler maps each valid search state to an executable solver. In effect, ADSL-PDE reshapes the search space: it removes large regions of invalid programs, increases the density of meaningful candidates, and preserves the compositional freedom needed to discover previously unseen designs. Solver evolution can thus operate over design decisions rather than code artifacts. Built on this representation, our evolutionary agent iteratively proposes, evaluates, and refines solver search states using empirical feedback. Across multiple PDE benchmarks, ADSL-PDE improves both search efficiency and optimization stability, achieving an improvement of more than 52% within the first ten evolution iterations. These results suggest a broader principle for LLM-driven auto-design: effective agents do not merely require stronger reasoning, but rather a search representation that concentrates exploration on valid and consequential decisions.

</details>


### [133] [Architectural Implications of Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04458)
*Jirong Yang,Peizhe Liu,Chaojie Zhang,Jovan Stojkovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对数据中心中的Agentic AI工作负载进行架构特征化研究，基于微软Azure的生产环境观测和开源框架的受控实验，揭示了agentic工作负载的执行碎片化、异构化以及CPU-GPU边界频繁跨越等特征，并提出了面向agentic服务器的原型系统Agora来动态利用空闲资源、优化GPU内存和调度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI正在数据中心兴起，但其架构影响尚未被探索。理解agentic工作负载的架构特征对设计高效服务器至关重要。

Method: 提出agentic工作流的分类法（taxonomy），结合微软Azure生产环境研究和开源框架受控研究，对agentic执行进行首次架构特征化；基于发现设计Agora原型，包括动态收割空闲CPU核、GPU内存超订与状态预取、按角色核池化与亲和感知调度等机制。

Result: agentic执行呈现碎片化和异构化，请求扩展为LLM推理、工具调用和编排决策的混合工作流，频繁跨越CPU-GPU边界。CPU处于关键路径上，负载呈低基数突刺形态，GPU利用率不均，任务和工具多样性进一步扩大资源需求波动。Agora通过动态资源回收、GPU内存超订和角色感知调度，在保护agent尾延迟的同时提升了利用率和吞吐量。

Conclusion: 传统统一服务器在架构上与agentic工作负载不匹配（碎片化执行导致CPU/GPU能力闲置、同质CPU配置低效、多agent复用共享核损害微架构局部性）。Agora验证了自适应机制的有效性，未来agentic服务器架构需要在资源编排、内存层次和调度局部性方面进行针对性设计。

Abstract: Agentic AI is emerging in datacenters, but its architectural implications remain unexplored. We organize agentic workflows in a taxonomy and present its first architectural characterization with a production study at Microsoft Azure and a controlled study of open-source frameworks. We show that agentic execution is fragmented and heterogeneous. Requests expand into a workflow of LLM inferences, tool invocations, and orchestration decisions that repeatedly cross the CPU-GPU boundary. Our taxonomy explains how this fragmentation turns into resource demand. As orchestration and tools run on the host, the CPU sits on the critical path. Execution structure sets the load over time, which stays low with sudden spikes. Model composition sets how evenly the workflow uses the GPUs. Diversity in tasks and tools widens this range even further. These characteristics expose architectural mismatches of conventional uniform servers. Fragmented execution strands CPU and GPU capacity despite bursty demand. Different software roles make homogeneous CPU provisioning inefficient. Finally, multiplexing many agents onto shared cores degrades microarchitectural locality. Guided by our findings, we derive implications for agentic servers and examine them through Agora, our prototype for commodity servers. Agora dynamically harvests idle CPU cores for co-located throughput work, while protecting agentic tail latency against tool spikes. It oversubscribes GPU memory by placing more agents on each GPU, prefetching the next agent's state to hide swap latency. To match the machine to the heterogeneous roles, Agora pools cores by role and applies affinity-aware scheduling to restore locality. It automatically tunes mechanisms to the workload. Agora improves utilization and server throughput while preserving agent tail latency. Our insights also identify key directions for future server architectures for agentic AI.

</details>


### [134] [CARGO-VL: Counterfactual Arbitration with Risk-Constrained Group Optimization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04509)
*De Jiang,Zhengyang Zhang,Kehong Yuan,Shaohua Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARGO-VL，一种群相对（group-relative）框架，通过将视觉语言系统中的证据状态（一致、仅图像正确、仅文本正确、两者均错）进行匹配变体联合优化，并引入转换奖励与原始对偶控制器，来增强多模态证据仲裁的可靠性，支持在冲突或不足证据时正确弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言系统将图像与检索文本结合，但二者可能相互矛盾或共同无法支持答案。可靠模型需要识别可信来源并在两者均不足时弃权。然而现有的后训练目标对实例独立打分，无法在反事实证据变化下强制执行连贯行为，导致模型在证据冲突场景中缺乏一致性保证。

Method: CARGO-VL采用群相对优化框架：将匹配的变体样本（覆盖A/V/T/N四种证据状态）打包为一组进行联合优化；目标函数耦合条件正确性奖励与转换奖励（包括答案不变性、来源等变性、答案到弃权的切换）；使用原始对偶控制器在"不安全答案"与"过度弃权"之间进行平衡；并构建XMC（eXtended Modal Conflict）四条件冲突训练资源。

Result: 在多个随机种子下，CARGO-VL在冲突处理、不支持答案规避和模态平衡方面均优于逐点（pointwise）基线方法。消融实验表明，关系性转换信号与自适应风险控制具有互补性收益。

Conclusion: 反事实一致性可作为可靠多模态证据仲裁的实用优化目标，CARGO-VL通过群相对奖励与自适应风险控制共同提升了模型在证据冲突与不足场景下的可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-language systems combine images with retrieved text, but these sources can disagree or jointly fail to support an answer. Reliable models must identify the trustworthy source and abstain when neither is adequate. Existing post-training objectives score instances independently and therefore do not enforce coherent behavior under counterfactual evidence changes. We introduce CARGO-VL, a group-relative framework that optimizes matched variants covering aligned, image-correct, text-correct, and both-wrong (A/V/T/N) evidence states as one bundle. Its objective couples condition-wise correctness with transition rewards for answer invariance, source equivariance, and answer-to-abstention switching, while a primal-dual controller balances unsafe answers against excessive deferral. We also contribute XMC (eXtended Modal Conflict), a four-condition conflict training resource, and evaluate transfer on CMC-Bench and Modality-Bias. Across multiple seeds, CARGO-VL improves conflict handling, unsupported-answer avoidance, and modality balance over pointwise baselines. Ablations identify complementary benefits from relational transition signals and adaptive risk control, supporting counterfactual consistency as a practical objective for reliable multimodal evidence arbitration.

</details>


### [135] [Leak-Resistant Unlearning: A New Benchmark for Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Consistency and Recovery Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04519)
*Haoting Qian,Qingjie Zhang,Zhicong Huang,Cheng Hong,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为\unlearning的新基准，用于评估大语言模型在不同多跳推理路径和恢复攻击下的知识遗忘鲁棒性。实验涉及3个模型、6种遗忘方法和2个数据集，结果表明现有方法在多跳推理路径和恢复攻击下均表现脆弱，并进一步探索了遗忘质量、鲁棒性和模型效用之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘基准主要包含单跳问题和少量多跳问题，存在两大挑战：(1) 知识并非孤立存在，多样的多跳推理路径可能比常规查询更容易引发知识泄漏；(2) 遗忘可能是脆弱的，遗忘的知识可以通过轻量级的遗忘后适应等恢复攻击被部分恢复，使得静态评估不足。

Method: 提出了\unlearning基准，涵盖多样的多跳推理路径和恢复攻击场景，并在3个模型、6种遗忘方法、2个精心构建的数据集上进行系统实验评估。

Result: 实验表明现有的遗忘方法对多跳推理路径和恢复攻击均表现脆弱，无法有效保证知识的鲁棒移除。

Conclusion: 研究进一步探索了遗忘质量、鲁棒性和模型效用三者之间的权衡关系，为LLM机器遗忘的鲁棒性评估提供了新视角和基准工具。

Abstract: Benchmarking machine unlearning methods is critical to understand whether sensitive knowledge is removed from large language models (LLMs) or not. Current unlearning benchmarks include mainly single-hop questions and a narrow set of multi-hop questions. Although effective, they still face two challenges. (1) Knowledge is not isolated, whereby diverse multi-hop reasoning paths can potentially induce knowledge leakage than normal queries. (2) Unlearning may be fragile: unlearned knowledge can be partially recovered through recovery attacks such as lightweight post-unlearning adaptation, making static evaluation insufficient. Therefore, in this paper, we introduce \unlearning as a novel benchmark to understand robust LLM knowledge removal across diverse reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We experiment with this benchmark on 3 models, 6 unlearning methods, and 2 carefully curated datasets. Results show that existing methods are vulnerable to multi-hop reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We further explore the trade-off among forget quality, robustness, and model utility for LLM unlearning.

</details>


### [136] [What Is a Skill Worth? Structure-Aware Shapley Valuation of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04562)
*Tao Li,Junfeng Liu,Qinghua Zhao,Yifan Li,Lei Wang,Bo Shao,Xuejun Liu,Linjun Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSV，一种结构感知的Shapley式框架，用于评估智能体技能内部单元（规则、示例、脚本、启发式）的价值，通过编译技能为单元、依赖和层级结构，结合配对删除和长度中立填充，实现有效反事实评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能越来越多地由自动化反馈循环优化，生成冗长的结构化产物，但其内部单元的价值不明确。现有数据或提示跨度评估方法无法处理技能单元的结构化特性：单元间存在依赖、属于文档层级、触发智能体行为且消耗有限提示上下文。

Method: SkillSV将技能编译为单元、依赖关系和层级结构，确保仅评估有效的反事实技能；采用配对删除和长度中立填充来分离内容价值与上下文成本；使用rollout预算估计器处理噪声智能体评估。

Result: 在四个智能体基准上验证了SkillSV的忠实性、可操作性和解释性：能够恢复单元交互、保持聚合技能提升，并指导安全剪枝和压缩。

Conclusion: SkillSV提供了首个结构感知的技能估值框架，能够准确评估智能体技能内部单元的价值，支持安全剪枝和压缩等实际应用。

Abstract: Agent skills are increasingly optimized by automated feedback loops, producing long structured artifacts whose internal value remains unclear. We study skill valuation: assigning credit to the internal units of a fixed skill, such as rules, examples, scripts, and heuristics, under a fixed agent and held-out task distribution. Skill valuation differs from data or prompt-span valuation because skill units are structured: they may depend on other units, belong to a document hierarchy, trigger agent behavior, and consume limited prompt context. We introduce SkillSV, a structure-aware Shapley-style framework for skill valuation. SkillSV compiles a skill into units, dependencies, and hierarchy, so that only valid counterfactual skills are evaluated. It uses paired deletion and length-neutral padding to separate content value from context cost, and estimates the resulting values with a rollout-budgeted estimator for noisy agent evaluations. On four agentic benchmarks, we assess the faithfulness, actionability, and explanation of SkillSV: it recovers unit interactions, preserves aggregate skill lift, and guides safe pruning and compression.

</details>


### [137] [Joint UAV Flight and Opportunistic Routing under Reinforcement Learning for Delay-Tolerant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04590)
*Xiao Wang,Shun-Ren Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JUROR框架，基于PPO联合优化无人机飞行路径与机会路由决策，采用集中训练-分散执行的factored POMDP架构，在延迟容忍网络中通过扩大未来接触机会和逐节点复制策略应对稀疏连接与拥塞，仿真优于PRoPHET和MaxProp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTN中稀疏连接、有限缓冲和TTL限制导致投递稀疏与拥塞，端到端性能严重下降，现有方法未联合优化路由与UAV飞行控制。

Method: 将问题建模为带序列运动-路由耦合和每步团队奖励的factored POMDP；采用集中训练-分散执行(CTDE)架构，分散actor基于局部观测决策，训练时critic利用全局统计信息，并引入可选的多时域热点预测器提供辅助监督；基于PPO框架实现。

Result: 在四种流量模式下，JUROR相对PRoPHET和MaxProp均取得有效性能增益，同时保持接触受限下的分散执行能力。

Conclusion: 联合优化UAV飞行与机会路由的CTDE框架能有效提升DTN性能，且分散执行机制保证了实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: The growing deployment of delay-tolerant networks (DTNs) has made store-carry-forward (SCF) communication indispensable under sparse connectivity.
  However, intermittent contacts, finite buffers, and limited message time-to-live (TTL) often give rise to sparse delivery and congestion, leading to substantial end-to-end performance degradation.
  To address this challenge, this study explores the joint optimization of decentralized opportunistic routing and controllable unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) flight, aiming to enlarge future contacts through discrete UAV headings while enabling per-node replication under contact-limited observations.
  Building upon this architecture, we study cooperative factored routing--UAV control under centralized training and decentralized execution (CTDE) and propose JUROR (Joint UAV flight and Opportunistic Routing, based on the proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework.
  In our design, we first cast the problem as a factored partially observable Markov decision process with sequential motion--routing coupling and a per-step team reward; subsequently, decentralized actors act on local observations while a training-time critic uses global statistics, and an optional multi-horizon hotspot predictor provides auxiliary supervision.
  Simulation results over four traffic modes demonstrate effective gains over PRoPHET and MaxProp, while retaining contact-limited decentralized execution.

</details>


### [138] [Agreement Before Diversity: Verification-First Complementarity for Heterogeneous Language-Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04618)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ABD（Agreement-Before-Diversity）方法，一个冻结的、无需标签的决策规则，用于在异构语言模型集成中决定何时用新生成的答案替代已有答案。核心思想是：锚点答案需被两个额外可信样本在固定等价关系下佐证才得以保留，否则被异构合成替代。作者证明了两个精确恒等式，分别刻画了相较无条件合成和从不合成的准确率差距的构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构语言模型集成扩展了候选答案空间，但缺乏一个原则性准则来判断新生成的答案何时应取代已有答案。现有方法将候选答案的潜力（headroom）与替换权威（replacement authority）混为一谈，需要一种将替换权威显式化、可审计的解耦机制。

Method: 提出ABD（Agreement-Before-Diversity）方法：这是一个冻结的、免标签的决策规则。给定锚点答案，若两个额外可信样本在固定等价关系下佐证该答案，则保留锚点；否则用异构合成答案替换。作者还证明了两个精确恒等式：其一说明相对无条件合成的准确率差距由协议覆盖率和锚点在受保护子集上的优势共同决定；其二说明相对从不合成的差距反映授权恢复与授权破坏之间的对比。该方法的期望推理成本约为8-5×覆盖率（次调用）。

Result: 在盲测精确ID评估下，ABD在完整LiveCodeBench-v6上达到59.43%（对比Single9的52.57%和HAC的52.00%，n=175），在未触碰的GPQA-Diamond分割上达到75.00%（两个对照组均为72.78%，n=180）。恒等式将聚合差异定位到可枚举的受保护层：LiveCodeBench上3个受保护案例中无不一致项，覆盖率先验约束该门控贡献不超过1.71个百分点；GPQA-Diamond上132例中13比8个不一致案例；冻结锚点扰动下71例中12比0个不一致案例。

Conclusion: 多样性提供潜力，验证结构提供权威。ABD通过解耦候选答案的潜力与替换权威，提供了一种可审计且理论上严谨的集成决策机制。

Abstract: Heterogeneous language-model ensembles expand the space of candidate responses, yet they lack a principled criterion for when a newly generated answer should supersede an already supported one. We decouple candidate headroom from replacement authority, rendering the latter as an explicit, auditable object. Our proposed method, Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD), is a frozen, label-free decision rule: an anchor answer is retained if two additional trusted samples corroborate it under a fixed equivalence relation; otherwise, it is replaced by a heterogeneous synthesis. For this gating mechanism, we prove two exact identities. The first shows that the accuracy gap relative to unconditional synthesis is determined jointly by the agreement coverage and the anchor's advantage on the protected subset. The second shows that the gap relative to never synthesizing reflects a contrast between authorized recovery and authorized destruction. Neither identity assumes independence or calibrated confidence, and the expected inference cost is approximately eight minus five times the coverage in number of calls. Under blind, exact-ID evaluation, ABD achieves 59.43% on the complete LiveCodeBench-v6 (vs. 52.57% for Single9 and 52.00% for HAC; n = 175) and 75.00% on an untouched GPQA-Diamond split (both controls at 72.78%; n = 180). Furthermore, these identities localize every aggregate difference to an enumerable protected stratum: no discordant items occur among the 3 protected cases on LiveCodeBench, where coverage bounds the gate's contribution to 1.71 points a priori; 13 versus 8 discordant cases among 132 on GPQA-Diamond; and 12 versus 0 among 71 under a frozen anchor perturbation. Diversity supplies potential; verification structure supplies authority.

</details>


### [139] [A/B Agent: A Self-Evolving Agent for Strategy Iteration in Industrial A/B Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04625)
*Zhuohang Jiang,Yuxin Chen,Yongsen Pan,Zheng Hu,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li,Hongyang Wang,Jun Wang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出A/B Agent，一个用于工业推荐策略优化的闭环A/B智能体系统。它通过层次化经验树组织历史策略知识、多路径Tree-RAG生成可执行策略，并利用线上A/B反馈驱动策略自进化，在真实短视频电商推荐系统中实现了4.829%的GMV提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业推荐策略调优依赖专家反复人工设计实验、配置和调参，流程繁琐且耗时。历史实验知识碎片化，难以通过人工进行系统化复用。现有RAG智能体虽能检索历史策略，但以扁平方式组织经验，忽略了业务场景、推荐阶段、优化目标和实验上下文之间的层次关系，导致检索不匹配、跨场景迁移有限，且无法通过连续A/B反馈持续优化策略和参数。

Method: 提出由三个紧密耦合的核心组件构成的A/B Agent框架：(1) 历史策略知识组织——将历史策略组织为层次化经验树；(2) 自主目标感知策略生成——通过多路径Tree-RAG检索可迁移证据，生成可执行策略；(3) 实验引导策略自进化——持续分析线上A/B反馈，引导自主调参并更新经验树实现自我进化。

Result: 在离线与线上评估中均展现出有效性。在真实短视频电商推荐系统中，GMV提升4.829%，且所有护栏指标均保持正向收益。

Conclusion: A/B Agent通过构建层次化经验知识库和闭环A/B反馈机制，实现了推荐策略的自主生成与持续进化，有效解决了传统人工调参效率低和知识复用困难的问题，为工业级推荐策略优化提供了一种可落地的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation strategy iteration heavily relies on large-scale A/B experimentation. Traditional tuning requires experts to repeatedly design strategies, configure experiments, analyze results, and adjust parameters, making the process labor-intensive and time-consuming. Meanwhile, valuable knowledge from historical experiments is often fragmented, making systematic reuse difficult through manual expert effort alone. Existing RAG agents partially alleviate this burden by retrieving prior strategies, but typically organize experience in a flat manner, overlooking the hierarchical relationships among business scenarios, recommendation stages, optimization objectives, and experimental contexts. This often results in mismatched retrieval and limited cross-scenario transfer, while preventing agents from continuously refining strategies and parameters through sequential A/B feedback. %
To address these limitations, we propose A/B Agent, a closed-loop A/B agent for industrial recommendation strategy optimization. The framework comprises three tightly coupled core components: Historical Strategy Knowledge Organization, Autonomous Target-Aware Strategy Generation, and Experiment-Guided Strategy Self-Evolution. It organizes historical strategies into a hierarchical experience tree, retrieves transferable evidence through multi-path Tree-RAG to generate executable strategies, and continuously analyzes online A/B feedback to guide autonomous tuning and update the experience tree for self-evolution. Extensive offline and online evaluations demonstrate its effectiveness, including a 4.829% improvement in GMV in a real-world short-video e-commerce recommendation system while maintaining positive gains across all guardrail metrics.

</details>


### [140] [AI Literacy for Legal Translation: Developing Digital Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04641)
*Łucja Biel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI对专业法律翻译的影响，并提出一个面向法律翻译职业的AI素养框架，包含基础、程序、批判和战略四个相互强化的维度，将AI素养视为法律翻译能力的横向组成部分，并讨论了相应的教学应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正深刻变革法律翻译行业，带来语言、技术、法律、伦理和认知等多维度的机遇与风险。然而，现有研究缺乏针对法律翻译职业特性的系统性AI素养框架，职业培训与教育亟待适应这一技术变革。

Method: 本文采用概念性论证方法，首先分析AI对法律翻译各层面的影响，随后提出包含基础、程序、批判和战略四个互相强化维度的AI素养框架，并将AI素养概念化为法律翻译能力的横向组成部分；最后提出将框架落地于法律翻译教育的课堂活动设计。

Result: 提出了一个四维度AI素养框架（基础、程序、批判、战略），论证AI不改变法律翻译的根本目标但要求扩展职业能力，并将AI素养定位为培养数字韧性的横向能力成分，同时给出了具体的教学实施建议。

Conclusion: 法律翻译从业者需要将AI素养融入其职业能力体系，通过教育引导未来译者以批判性、负责任且符合专业标准的方式整合AI工具，从而在技术变革中保持专业核心竞争力。

Abstract: Generative AI is transforming legal translation by introducing opportunities alongside linguistic, technical, legal, ethical and cognitive risks. This chapter examines the implications of AI for professional legal translation and proposes an AI literacy framework tailored to the profession. It argues that AI does not change the fundamental objectives of legal translation but requires an extension of professional competence through AI literacy. The proposed framework comprises four mutually reinforcing dimensions, foundational, procedural, critical and strategic, and conceptualises AI literacy as a transversal component of legal translation competence that fosters digital resilience. It further discusses the pedagogical implications of this framework by proposing classroom activities designed to develop AI literacy in legal translator education, enabling future translators to integrate AI critically, responsibly and in accordance with professional standards.

</details>


### [141] [Calibrating Artificial Guilt: Neurally Grounded Reward Shaping for Prosocial Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04663)
*Aaditya Mehta,Arya Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用人类fMRI数据（SoDec数据集，40名参与者）校准合作多智能体强化学习中的内疚信号权重，将其嵌入两智能体Social Lottery环境中的PPO智能体，发现神经校准的塑造项最接近人类行为（KL=0.0012）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作多智能体强化学习中，社会项通常被添加到个体奖励中，但这些项的规模往往依赖人工设定。作者希望探究能否从人类神经和行为数据中校准内疚信号，并将其迁移到人工智能体，从而用定量的人类先验约束亲社会奖励塑造。
